  set(${extensions}
    qtinsighttracker
    qtpdf
    qtwebengine

    PARENT_SCOPE
  )
endfunction()

function(qt_maintenance_tool_get_standalone_addons standalone_addons_list)
  set(${standalone_addons_list}
    qtquick3d
    qt5compat
    qtshadertools
    qtquicktimeline

    PARENT_SCOPE
  )
endfunction()

function(qt_maintenance_tool_install qt_major_version qt_package_list)
  if (QT_QMAKE_EXECUTABLE MATCHES ".*/(.*)/(.*)/bin/qmake")
    set(qt_version_number ${CMAKE_MATCH_1})
    string(REPLACE "." "" qt_version_number_dotless ${qt_version_number})
    set(qt_build_flavor ${CMAKE_MATCH_2})

    set(additional_addons "")
    qt_maintenance_tool_get_extensions(__qt_extensions)
    if (qt_version_number VERSION_LESS 6.8.0)
      set(additional_addons ${__qt_extensions})
    endif()
    if (WIN32)
      list(APPEND additional_addons qtactiveqt)
    endif()
    if (UNIX AND NOT APPLE)
      list(APPEND additional_addons qtwaylandcompositor)
    endif()

    qt_maintenance_tool_get_component_platform(${qt_build_flavor} component_platform)
    if (NOT component_platform)
      message(STATUS
        "Qt Creator: Could not find the component platform for '${qt_build_flavor}' "
        "required by the MaintenanceTool. This can happen with a non Qt SDK installation "
        "(e.g. system Linux or macOS homebrew).")
      return()
    endif()

    set(installer_component_list "")
    foreach (qt_package_name IN LISTS qt_package_list)
      string(TOLOWER "${qt_package_name}" qt_package_name_lowercase)

      qt_maintenance_tool_get_addons(__qt_addons)
      if (qt_version_number VERSION_LESS 6.8.0)
        qt_maintenance_tool_get_standalone_addons(__standalone_addons)
        foreach(standalone_addon IN LISTS __standalone_addons)
          list(REMOVE_ITEM __qt_addons ${standalone_addon})
        endforeach()
      endif()

      # Is the package an addon?
      set(install_addon FALSE)
      foreach(addon IN LISTS __qt_addons additional_addons)
        string(REGEX MATCH "^${addon}$" is_addon "qt${qt_package_name_lowercase}")
        if (is_addon)
          list(
            APPEND installer_component_list
            "qt.qt${qt_major_version}.${qt_version_number_dotless}.addons.${addon}"
          )
          set(install_addon TRUE)
          break()
        endif()
      endforeach()

      if (NOT install_addon)
        set(install_extension FALSE)
        foreach(extension IN LISTS __qt_extensions)
          string(REGEX MATCH "^${extension}$" is_extension "qt${qt_package_name_lowercase}")
          if (is_extension)
            list(
              APPEND installer_component_list
              "extensions.${extension}.${qt_version_number_dotless}.${component_platform}"
            )
            set(install_extension TRUE)
            break()
          endif()
        endforeach()

        if (NOT install_extension)
          set(install_standalone_addon FALSE)
          foreach(standalone_addon IN LISTS __standalone_addons)
            string(REGEX MATCH "^${standalone_addon}$" is_standalone_addon "qt${qt_package_name_lowercase}")
            if (is_standalone_addon)
              list(
                APPEND installer_component_list
                "qt.qt${qt_major_version}.${qt_version_number_dotless}.${standalone_addon}"
              )
              set(install_standalone_addon TRUE)
              break()
            endif()
          endforeach()

          if(NOT install_standalone_addon)
            # Install the Desktop package
            list(
              APPEND installer_component_list
              "qt.qt${qt_major_version}.${qt_version_number_dotless}.${component_platform}"
            )
          endif()
        endif()
      endif()
    endforeach()

    # THIS MESSAGE IS PARSED IN THE CMakeOutputParser
    message(WARNING "Qt packages are missing: ${qt_version_number}, ${qt_build_flavor}, ${installer_component_list}\n"
                    "If you are using the Qt Online Installer, check the Issues view in Qt Creator "
                    "for a link that installs the missing component.")
  endif()
endfunction()

macro(qt_maintenance_tool_dependency method package_name)
  # Work around upstream cmake issue: https://gitlab.kitware.com/cmake/cmake/-/issues/27169
  if(ANDROID
      AND CMAKE_VERSION VERSION_GREATER_EQUAL 3.29
      AND NOT ANDROID_USE_LEGACY_TOOLCHAIN_FILE
      AND NOT CMAKE_CXX_COMPILER_CLANG_SCAN_DEPS
      AND NOT QT_NO_SET_MAINTENANCE_TOOL_PROVIDER_POLICY_CMP0155
    )
    message(DEBUG
      "Setting policy CMP0155 to OLD inside qt_maintenance_tool_dependency to avoid "
      "issues with not being able to find the Threads package when targeting Android with "
      "cmake_minimum_required(3.29) and CMAKE_CXX_STANDARD >= 20."
    )
    # We don't create an explicit policy stack entry before changing the policy, because setting
    # the policy value bubbles it up to the parent find_package() scope, which is fine, that's
    # exactly the scope we want to modify.
    cmake_policy(SET CMP0155 OLD)
  endif()

  if (${package_name} MATCHES "^Qt([5-9])(.*)$")
    set(__qt_dependency_qt_major_version ${CMAKE_MATCH_1})
    set(__qt_dependency_qt_package_name ${CMAKE_MATCH_2})

    # https://cmake.org/cmake/help/latest/command/find_package.html
    set(__qt_dependency_options
      CONFIG NO_MODULE MODULE REQUIRED EXACT QUIET GLOBAL NO_POLICY_SCOPE NO_DEFAULT_PATH NO_PACKAGE_ROOT_PATH
      NO_CMAKE_PATH NO_CMAKE_ENVIRONMENT_PATH NO_SYSTEM_ENVIRONMENT_PATH NO_CMAKE_PACKAGE_REGISTRY
      NO_CMAKE_SYSTEM_PATH NO_CMAKE_INSTALL_PREFIX NO_CMAKE_SYSTEM_PACKAGE_REGISTRY CMAKE_FIND_ROOT_PATH_BOTH
      ONLY_CMAKE_FIND_ROOT_PATH NO_CMAKE_FIND_ROOT_PATH
    )
    set(__qt_dependency_oneValueArgs REGISTRY_VIEW)
    set(__qt_dependency_multiValueArgs COMPONENTS OPTIONAL_COMPONENTS NAMES CONFIGS HINTS PATHS PATH_SUFFIXES)
    cmake_parse_arguments(__qt_dependency_arg "${__qt_dependency_options}" "${__qt_dependency_oneValueArgs}" "${__qt_dependency_multiValueArgs}" ${ARGN})

    if (__qt_dependency_arg_REQUIRED AND __qt_dependency_arg_COMPONENTS)
      # Install missing COMPONENTS.
      set(__qt_dependency_pkgs_to_install "")
      foreach(pkg IN LISTS __qt_dependency_arg_COMPONENTS)
        find_package(Qt${__qt_dependency_qt_major_version}${pkg}
          PATHS ${CMAKE_PREFIX_PATH} ${CMAKE_MODULE_PATH} NO_DEFAULT_PATH BYPASS_PROVIDER QUIET)
        if (NOT Qt${__qt_dependency_qt_major_version}${pkg}_FOUND)
          list(APPEND __qt_dependency_pkgs_to_install ${pkg})
        endif()
      endforeach()
      if (__qt_dependency_pkgs_to_install)
        qt_maintenance_tool_install("${__qt_dependency_qt_major_version}" "${__qt_dependency_pkgs_to_install}")
      endif()
    elseif(__qt_dependency_arg_REQUIRED AND NOT __qt_dependency_qt_package_name)
      # Install the Desktop package if Qt::Core is missing
      find_package(Qt${__qt_dependency_qt_major_version}Core
        PATHS ${CMAKE_PREFIX_PATH} ${CMAKE_MODULE_PATH} NO_DEFAULT_PATH BYPASS_PROVIDER QUIET)
      if (NOT Qt${__qt_dependency_qt_major_version}$Core_FOUND)
        qt_maintenance_tool_install("${__qt_dependency_qt_major_version}" Core)
      endif()
    endif()

    find_package(${package_name} ${ARGN}
      PATHS ${CMAKE_PREFIX_PATH} ${CMAKE_MODULE_PATH} NO_DEFAULT_PATH BYPASS_PROVIDER QUIET)
    if (NOT ${package_name}_FOUND AND __qt_dependency_arg_REQUIRED)
      qt_maintenance_tool_install("${__qt_dependency_qt_major_version}" "${__qt_dependency_qt_package_name}")
      find_package(${package_name} ${ARGN} BYPASS_PROVIDER)
    endif()
  else()
    find_package(${package_name} ${ARGN} BYPASS_PROVIDER)
  endif()
endmacro()

cmake_language(
    SET_DEPENDENCY_PROVIDER qt_maintenance_tool_dependency
    SUPPORTED_METHODS FIND_PACKAGE
)

if (EXISTS "${CMAKE_SOURCE_DIR}/QtCreatorPackageManager.cmake")
  include("${CMAKE_SOURCE_DIR}/QtCreatorPackageManager.cmake")
endif()

if (NOT QT_CREATOR_ENABLE_PACKAGE_MANAGER_SETUP)
  return()
endif()

macro(qtc_auto_setup_compiler_standard toolchainFile)
  foreach(lang_var C CXX CUDA OBJC OBJCXX)
    foreach(prop_var STANDARD STANDARD_REQUIRED EXTENSIONS)
      if (CMAKE_${lang_var}_${prop_var})
        file(APPEND "${toolchainFile}"
             "set(CMAKE_${lang_var}_${prop_var} ${CMAKE_${lang_var}_${prop_var}})\n")
      endif()
    endforeach()
  endforeach()

  # If the ENV{ANDROID_NDK_HOME} exists pass it via the ANDROID_NDK variable
  if (DEFINED ENV{ANDROID_NDK_HOME})
      set(ANDROID_NDK "$ENV{ANDROID_NDK_HOME}")
  endif()

  # Forward important CMake variables to the package manager in the toolchain file
  foreach(fwd_var CMAKE_MSVC_RUNTIME_LIBRARY CMAKE_SYSROOT
                  CMAKE_OSX_SYSROOT CMAKE_OSX_ARCHITECTURES
                  ANDROID_ABI ANDROID_NDK)
    if (${fwd_var})
      file(APPEND "${toolchainFile}"
          "set(${fwd_var} \"${${fwd_var}}\")\n")
    endif()
  endforeach()
endmacro()

#
# conan
#
macro(qtc_auto_setup_conan)
  foreach(file conanfile.txt conanfile.py)
    if (EXISTS "${CMAKE_SOURCE_DIR}/${file}")
      set(conanfile_txt "${CMAKE_SOURCE_DIR}/${file}")
      break()
    endif()
  endforeach()

  if (conanfile_txt AND NOT QT_CREATOR_SKIP_CONAN_SETUP)
    option(QT_CREATOR_SKIP_CONAN_SETUP "Skip Qt Creator's conan package manager auto-setup" OFF)
    set(QT_CREATOR_CONAN_BUILD_POLICY "missing" CACHE STRING "Qt Creator's conan package manager auto-setup build policy. This is used for the BUILD property of cmake_conan_run")

    set_property(
      DIRECTORY "${CMAKE_SOURCE_DIR}"
      APPEND
      PROPERTY CMAKE_CONFIGURE_DEPENDS "${conanfile_txt}")

    find_program(conan_program conan)
    if (NOT conan_program)
      message(WARNING "Qt Creator: conan executable not found. "
                      "Package manager auto-setup will be skipped. "
                      "To disable this warning set QT_CREATOR_SKIP_CONAN_SETUP to ON.")
      return()
    endif()
    execute_process(COMMAND ${conan_program} --version
      RESULT_VARIABLE result_code
      OUTPUT_VARIABLE conan_version_output
      ERROR_VARIABLE conan_version_output)
    if (NOT result_code EQUAL 0)
      message(FATAL_ERROR "conan --version failed='${result_code}: ${conan_version_output}")
    endif()

    string(REGEX REPLACE ".*Conan version ([0-9].[0-9]).*" "\\1" conan_version "${conan_version_output}")

    set(conanfile_timestamp_file "${CMAKE_BINARY_DIR}/conan-dependencies/conanfile.timestamp")
    file(TIMESTAMP "${conanfile_txt}" conanfile_timestamp)

    set(do_conan_installation ON)
    if (EXISTS "${conanfile_timestamp_file}")
      file(READ "${conanfile_timestamp_file}" old_conanfile_timestamp)
      if ("${conanfile_timestamp}" STREQUAL "${old_conanfile_timestamp}")
        set(do_conan_installation OFF)
      endif()
    endif()

    set(conanfile_build_policy_file "${CMAKE_BINARY_DIR}/conan-dependencies/conanfile.buildpolicy")
    if (EXISTS "${conanfile_build_policy_file}")
      file(READ "${conanfile_build_policy_file}" build_policy)
      if (NOT "${build_policy}" STREQUAL "${QT_CREATOR_CONAN_BUILD_POLICY}")
        set(do_conan_installation ON)
      endif()
    endif()

    if (do_conan_installation)
      message(STATUS "Qt Creator: conan package manager auto-setup. "
                     "Skip this step by setting QT_CREATOR_SKIP_CONAN_SETUP to ON.")

      file(COPY "${conanfile_txt}" DESTINATION "${CMAKE_BINARY_DIR}/conan-dependencies/")

      # conanfile should have a generator specified, when both file and conan_install
      # specifcy the CMakeDeps or CMakeConfigDeps generator, conan_install will issue an error
      file(READ "${conanfile_txt}" conanfile_text_content)
      if (NOT "${conanfile_text_content}" MATCHES ".*(CMakeDeps|CMakeConfigDeps).*")
        message(WARNING "Qt Creator: CMakeDeps or CMakeConfigDeps generator was not defined in the conanfile.")
      endif()

      file(WRITE "${CMAKE_BINARY_DIR}/conan-dependencies/toolchain.cmake" "
        set(CMAKE_C_COMPILER \"${CMAKE_C_COMPILER}\")
        set(CMAKE_CXX_COMPILER \"${CMAKE_CXX_COMPILER}\")
        ")
      qtc_auto_setup_compiler_standard("${CMAKE_BINARY_DIR}/conan-dependencies/toolchain.cmake")

      if (CMAKE_TOOLCHAIN_FILE)
        file(APPEND "${CMAKE_BINARY_DIR}/conan-dependencies/toolchain.cmake"
          "include(\"${CMAKE_TOOLCHAIN_FILE}\")\n")
      endif()

      file(WRITE "${CMAKE_BINARY_DIR}/conan-dependencies/CMakeLists.txt" "
        cmake_minimum_required(VERSION 3.15)

        unset(CMAKE_PROJECT_INCLUDE_BEFORE CACHE)
        project(conan-setup)

        if (${conan_version} VERSION_GREATER_EQUAL 2.0)
          set(CONAN_COMMAND \"${conan_program}\")
          include(\"${CMAKE_CURRENT_LIST_DIR}/conan_provider.cmake\")
          conan_profile_detect_default()
          detect_host_profile(\"${CMAKE_BINARY_DIR}/conan-dependencies/conan_host_profile\")

          set(build_types \${CMAKE_BUILD_TYPE})
          if (CMAKE_CONFIGURATION_TYPES)
            set(build_types \${CMAKE_CONFIGURATION_TYPES})
          endif()

          foreach(type \${build_types})
            conan_install(
              -pr \"${CMAKE_BINARY_DIR}/conan-dependencies/conan_host_profile\"
              --build=${QT_CREATOR_CONAN_BUILD_POLICY}
              -s build_type=\${type}
            )
          endforeach()

          get_property(CONAN_INSTALL_SUCCESS GLOBAL PROPERTY CONAN_INSTALL_SUCCESS)
          if (CONAN_INSTALL_SUCCESS)
            get_property(CONAN_GENERATORS_FOLDER GLOBAL PROPERTY CONAN_GENERATORS_FOLDER)
            file(TO_CMAKE_PATH \"\${CONAN_GENERATORS_FOLDER}\" CONAN_GENERATORS_FOLDER)
            file(WRITE \"${CMAKE_BINARY_DIR}/conan-dependencies/conan_paths.cmake\" \"
              list(PREPEND CMAKE_PREFIX_PATH \\\"\${CONAN_GENERATORS_FOLDER}\\\")
              list(PREPEND CMAKE_MODULE_PATH \\\"\${CONAN_GENERATORS_FOLDER}\\\")
              list(PREPEND CMAKE_FIND_ROOT_PATH \\\"\${CONAN_GENERATORS_FOLDER}\\\")
              list(REMOVE_DUPLICATES CMAKE_PREFIX_PATH)
              list(REMOVE_DUPLICATES CMAKE_MODULE_PATH)
              list(REMOVE_DUPLICATES CMAKE_FIND_ROOT_PATH)
              set(CMAKE_PREFIX_PATH \\\"\\\${CMAKE_PREFIX_PATH}\\\" CACHE STRING \\\"\\\" FORCE)
              set(CMAKE_MODULE_PATH \\\"\\\${CMAKE_MODULE_PATH}\\\" CACHE STRING \\\"\\\" FORCE)
              set(CMAKE_FIND_ROOT_PATH \\\"\\\${CMAKE_FIND_ROOT_PATH}\\\" CACHE STRING \\\"\\\" FORCE)
            \")
          endif()
        else()
          include(\"${CMAKE_CURRENT_LIST_DIR}/conan.cmake\")
          conan_cmake_run(
            CONANFILE \"${conanfile_txt}\"
            INSTALL_FOLDER \"${CMAKE_BINARY_DIR}/conan-dependencies\"
            GENERATORS cmake_paths cmake_find_package json
            BUILD ${QT_CREATOR_CONAN_BUILD_POLICY}
            ENV CONAN_CMAKE_TOOLCHAIN_FILE=\"${CMAKE_BINARY_DIR}/conan-dependencies/toolchain.cmake\"
          )
        endif()
      ")

      if (NOT DEFINED CMAKE_BUILD_TYPE AND NOT DEFINED CMAKE_CONFIGURATION_TYPES)
          set(CMAKE_CONFIGURATION_TYPES "Debug;Release")
      endif()

      execute_process(COMMAND ${CMAKE_COMMAND}
        -S "${CMAKE_BINARY_DIR}/conan-dependencies/"
        -B "${CMAKE_BINARY_DIR}/conan-dependencies/build"
        -C "${CMAKE_BINARY_DIR}/qtcsettings.cmake"
        -D "CMAKE_TOOLCHAIN_FILE=${CMAKE_BINARY_DIR}/conan-dependencies/toolchain.cmake"
        -G ${CMAKE_GENERATOR}
        -D CMAKE_BUILD_TYPE=${CMAKE_BUILD_TYPE}
        -D "CMAKE_CONFIGURATION_TYPES=${CMAKE_CONFIGURATION_TYPES}"
        RESULT_VARIABLE result
      )
      if (result EQUAL 0)
        file(WRITE "${conanfile_timestamp_file}" "${conanfile_timestamp}")
        file(WRITE "${conanfile_build_policy_file}" ${QT_CREATOR_CONAN_BUILD_POLICY})
      else()
        message(WARNING "Qt Creator's conan package manager auto-setup failed. Consider setting "
                        "QT_CREATOR_SKIP_CONAN_SETUP to ON and reconfigure to skip this step.")
        return()
      endif()
    endif()

    include("${CMAKE_BINARY_DIR}/conan-dependencies/conan_paths.cmake")
  endif()
  unset(conanfile_txt)
endmacro()
qtc_auto_setup_conan()

#
# vcpkg
#
macro(qtc_auto_setup_vcpkg)
  if (EXISTS "${CMAKE_SOURCE_DIR}/vcpkg.json" AND NOT QT_CREATOR_SKIP_VCPKG_SETUP)
    option(QT_CREATOR_SKIP_VCPKG_SETUP "Skip Qt Creator's vcpkg package manager auto-setup" OFF)

    set_property(
      DIRECTORY "${CMAKE_SOURCE_DIR}"
      APPEND
      PROPERTY CMAKE_CONFIGURE_DEPENDS "${CMAKE_SOURCE_DIR}/vcpkg.json")

    find_program(vcpkg_program vcpkg
      PATHS ${CMAKE_SOURCE_DIR}/vcpkg ${CMAKE_SOURCE_DIR}/3rdparty/vcpkg $ENV{VCPKG_ROOT}
      NO_DEFAULT_PATH
    )
    if (NOT vcpkg_program)
      message(WARNING "Qt Creator: vcpkg executable not found. "
                      "Package manager auto-setup will be skipped. "
                      "To disable this warning set QT_CREATOR_SKIP_VCPKG_SETUP to ON.")
      return()
    endif()
    execute_process(COMMAND ${vcpkg_program} version
      RESULT_VARIABLE result_code
      OUTPUT_VARIABLE vcpkg_version_output
      ERROR_VARIABLE vcpkg_version_output)
    if (NOT result_code EQUAL 0)
      message(FATAL_ERROR "vcpkg version failed='${result_code}: ${vcpkg_version_output}")
    endif()

    # Resolve any symlinks
    get_filename_component(vpkg_program_real_path ${vcpkg_program} REALPATH)
    get_filename_component(vpkg_root ${vpkg_program_real_path} DIRECTORY)

    if (NOT EXISTS "${CMAKE_BINARY_DIR}/vcpkg-dependencies/toolchain.cmake")
      message(STATUS "Qt Creator: vcpkg package manager auto-setup. "
                     "Skip this step by setting QT_CREATOR_SKIP_VCPKG_SETUP to ON.")

      file(WRITE "${CMAKE_BINARY_DIR}/vcpkg-dependencies/toolchain.cmake" "
        set(CMAKE_C_COMPILER \"${CMAKE_C_COMPILER}\")
        set(CMAKE_CXX_COMPILER \"${CMAKE_CXX_COMPILER}\")
        ")
      qtc_auto_setup_compiler_standard("${CMAKE_BINARY_DIR}/vcpkg-dependencies/toolchain.cmake")

      # If set explicitly in Qt Creator's CMake parameters, just set it in the toolchain
      if (VCPKG_TARGET_TRIPLET)
        file(APPEND "${CMAKE_BINARY_DIR}/vcpkg-dependencies/toolchain.cmake"
          "set(VCPKG_TARGET_TRIPLET \"${VCPKG_TARGET_TRIPLET}\")\n")
      endif()

      if (CMAKE_TOOLCHAIN_FILE AND NOT
          CMAKE_TOOLCHAIN_FILE STREQUAL "${CMAKE_BINARY_DIR}/vcpkg-dependencies/toolchain.cmake")
        file(APPEND "${CMAKE_BINARY_DIR}/vcpkg-dependencies/toolchain.cmake"
          "include(\"${CMAKE_TOOLCHAIN_FILE}\")\n")
      endif()

      file(APPEND "${CMAKE_BINARY_DIR}/vcpkg-dependencies/toolchain.cmake" [=[
        if (VCPKG_TARGET_TRIPLET)
          set(vcpkg_triplet ${VCPKG_TARGET_TRIPLET})
        else()
          if (ANDROID_ABI)
            if (ANDROID_ABI STREQUAL "armeabi-v7a")
              set(vcpkg_triplet arm-neon-android)
            elseif (ANDROID_ABI STREQUAL "arm64-v8a")
              set(vcpkg_triplet arm64-android)
            elseif (ANDROID_ABI STREQUAL "x86")
                set(vcpkg_triplet x86-android)
            elseif (ANDROID_ABI STREQUAL "x86_64")
                set(vcpkg_triplet x64-android)
            else()
                message(FATAL_ERROR "Unsupported Android ABI: ${ANDROID_ABI}")
            endif()
            set(ENV{ANDROID_NDK_HOME} "${ANDROID_NDK}")
          elseif (WIN32)
            if ("$ENV{PROCESSOR_ARCHITECTURE}" STREQUAL "ARM64")
              set(vcpkg_triplet arm64-mingw-static)
            else()
              set(vcpkg_triplet x64-mingw-static)
            endif()
            if (CMAKE_CXX_COMPILER MATCHES ".*/(.*)/cl.exe")
              string(TOLOWER ${CMAKE_MATCH_1} host_arch_lowercase)
              set(vcpkg_triplet ${host_arch_lowercase}-windows)
            endif()
          elseif(APPLE)
            # We're too early to use CMAKE_HOST_SYSTEM_PROCESSOR
            execute_process(
              COMMAND uname -m
              OUTPUT_VARIABLE __apple_host_system_processor
              OUTPUT_STRIP_TRAILING_WHITESPACE)
            if (__apple_host_system_processor MATCHES "arm64")
              set(vcpkg_triplet arm64-osx)
            else()
              set(vcpkg_triplet x64-osx)
            endif()
          else()
            # We're too early to use CMAKE_HOST_SYSTEM_PROCESSOR
            execute_process(
              COMMAND uname -m
              OUTPUT_VARIABLE __linux_host_system_processor
              OUTPUT_STRIP_TRAILING_WHITESPACE)
            if (__linux_host_system_processor MATCHES "aarch64")
              set(vcpkg_triplet arm64-linux)
            else()
              set(vcpkg_triplet x64-linux)
            endif()
          endif()
        endif()

        set(VCPKG_TARGET_TRIPLET ${vcpkg_triplet})
      ]=])

      file(APPEND "${CMAKE_BINARY_DIR}/vcpkg-dependencies/toolchain.cmake" "
        include(\"${vpkg_root}/scripts/buildsystems/vcpkg.cmake\")
      ")
    endif()

    set(CMAKE_TOOLCHAIN_FILE "${CMAKE_BINARY_DIR}/vcpkg-dependencies/toolchain.cmake" CACHE PATH "" FORCE)

    # Save CMAKE_PREFIX_PATH and CMAKE_MODULE_PATH as cache variables
    if (CMAKE_VERSION GREATER_EQUAL "3.19")
      cmake_language(DEFER CALL list REMOVE_DUPLICATES CMAKE_PREFIX_PATH)
      cmake_language(DEFER CALL list REMOVE_DUPLICATES CMAKE_MODULE_PATH)
      cmake_language(DEFER CALL set CMAKE_PREFIX_PATH "${CMAKE_PREFIX_PATH}" CACHE STRING "" FORCE)
      cmake_language(DEFER CALL set CMAKE_MODULE_PATH "${CMAKE_MODULE_PATH}" CACHE STRING "" FORCE)
    endif()
  endif()
endmacro()
qtc_auto_setup_vcpkg()

#

#
if (CMAKE_VERSION GREATER_EQUAL "3.19" AND NOT QTCREATOR_CMAKE_HELPER_DIRECTORY)
  set(QTCREATOR_CMAKE_HELPER_DIRECTORY "${CMAKE_CURRENT_LIST_DIR}" CACHE INTERNAL "")
  cmake_language(DEFER CALL
    file CHMOD "${QTCREATOR_CMAKE_HELPER_DIRECTORY}"
      DIRECTORY_PERMISSIONS
        OWNER_READ OWNER_WRITE OWNER_EXECUTE
        GROUP_READ GROUP_EXECUTE
        WORLD_READ WORLD_EXECUTE)
  cmake_language(DEFER CALL
    file CHMOD_RECURSE "${QTCREATOR_CMAKE_HELPER_DIRECTORY}"
      FILE_PERMISSIONS
        OWNER_READ OWNER_WRITE
        GROUP_READ
        WORLD_READ)
endif()

#
# Set a better default value for CMAKE_INSTALL_PREFIX
#
function(qtc_modify_default_install_prefix)
  # If at configure time the user didn't specify a CMAKE_INSTALL_PREFIX variable
  # Modules/CMakeGenericSystem.cmake will set a default value
  # to CMAKE_INSTALL_PREFIX and set CMAKE_INSTALL_PREFIX_INITIALIZED_TO_DEFAULT to ON

  # In practice there are cases when CMAKE_INSTALL_PREFIX_INITIALIZED_TO_DEFAULT is
  # set to ON and a custom CMAKE_INSTALL_PREFIX is set

  # Do the original CMAKE_INSTALL_PREFIX detection
  if(CMAKE_HOST_UNIX)
    set(original_cmake_install_prefix "/usr/local")
  else()
    GetDefaultWindowsPrefixBase(CMAKE_GENERIC_PROGRAM_FILES)
    set(original_cmake_install_prefix
      "${CMAKE_GENERIC_PROGRAM_FILES}/${PROJECT_NAME}")
    unset(CMAKE_GENERIC_PROGRAM_FILES)
  endif()

  # When the user code didn't modify the CMake set CMAKE_INSTALL_PREFIX
  # then set the "/tmp" better value for CMAKE_INSTALL_PREFIX
  if (CMAKE_INSTALL_PREFIX_INITIALIZED_TO_DEFAULT AND
      CMAKE_INSTALL_PREFIX STREQUAL "${original_cmake_install_prefix}")
    set_property(CACHE CMAKE_INSTALL_PREFIX PROPERTY VALUE "/tmp")
  endif()
endfunction()
if (CMAKE_VERSION GREATER_EQUAL "3.19")
  cmake_language(DEFER CALL qtc_modify_default_install_prefix)
endif()

# Store the C/C++ object output extension
if (CMAKE_VERSION GREATER_EQUAL "3.19")
  cmake_language(DEFER CALL set CMAKE_C_OUTPUT_EXTENSION "${CMAKE_C_OUTPUT_EXTENSION}" CACHE STRING "" FORCE)
  cmake_language(DEFER CALL set CMAKE_CXX_OUTPUT_EXTENSION "${CMAKE_CXX_OUTPUT_EXTENSION}" CACHE STRING "" FORCE)
endif()

endif()

# MaintenanceTool
#
if (QT_CREATOR_ENABLE_MAINTENANCE_TOOL_PROVIDER)
  function(qtc_maintenance_provider_missing_variable_message variable)
    message(STATUS "Qt Creator: ${variable} was not set. "
                  "Qt MaintenanceTool cannot be used to install missing Qt modules that you specify in find_package(). "
                  "To disable this message set QT_CREATOR_ENABLE_MAINTENANCE_TOOL_PROVIDER to OFF.")
  endfunction()

  if (NOT QT_QMAKE_EXECUTABLE)
    qtc_maintenance_provider_missing_variable_message(QT_QMAKE_EXECUTABLE)
    return()
  endif()

  if (CMAKE_VERSION GREATER_EQUAL "3.24")
    list(APPEND CMAKE_PROJECT_TOP_LEVEL_INCLUDES ${CMAKE_CURRENT_LIST_DIR}/maintenance_tool_provider.cmake)
  else()
    message(WARNING "Qt Creator: CMake version 3.24 is needed for MaintenanceTool find_package() provider. "
                    "To disable this warning set QT_CREATOR_ENABLE_MAINTENANCE_TOOL_PROVIDER to OFF.")
  endif()
endif()

#pragma once
#include <QWidget>
#include <QStringList>

class QComboBox;
class QLabel;
class QPushButton;
class QStackedWidget;
class QFrame;
class CameraView;
class DropZone;
class SegBar;

// Screens 2-4 – "Decode Barcode": camera tab, upload tab and the "Scan Logs" overview.
class DecodePage : public QWidget
{
    Q_OBJECT
public:
    explicit DecodePage(QWidget *parent = nullptr);

    void setTab(int index); // 0 camera, 1 upload, 2 scan logs

    // ---- hooks for the backend ----
    void setCameraList(const QStringList &names);
    void setCameraActive(bool on);                  // sync buttons/viewport with the real camera state
    void setCameraMessage(const QString &text, bool error = false);   // hint line under the viewport
    bool cameraActive() const { return m_streaming; }
    void stopCamera();                              // stops (and emits cameraStopRequested) if running
    CameraView *cameraView() const { return m_camera; }
    void showResult(const QString &code); // success state on every screen
    // "Could not read barcode"; reason / errCode are optional (defaults keep old calls working)
    void showFailure(const QString &reason = QString(), const QString &errCode = QString());
    void resetResult();

signals:
    void cameraStartRequested(int cameraIndex);
    void cameraStopRequested();
    void decodeImageRequested(const QString &path);
    void generateFromCodeRequested(const QString &code);

private:
    struct ResultUi
    {
        QLabel *badge = nullptr;
        QLabel *code = nullptr;
        QPushButton *copy = nullptr;
        QFrame *fail = nullptr;
        bool detectedWording = false; // "Barcode Detected" instead of "Success"
        const char *okKind = "tint";
    };

    QWidget *buildCameraPage();
    QWidget *buildUploadPage();
    QWidget *buildLogsPage();
    SegBar *makeTabs(int current);
    QFrame *makeFailBox();
    void applyResult(const ResultUi &r, const QString &code);
    void loadImage(const QString &path);

    QStackedWidget *m_stack;
    // camera
    QComboBox *m_cameraBox = nullptr;
    QPushButton *m_stream = nullptr, *m_freeze = nullptr;
    CameraView *m_camera = nullptr;
    QLabel *m_camHint = nullptr;
    bool m_streaming = false;
    // upload
    QFrame *m_loaded = nullptr;
    QLabel *m_thumb = nullptr, *m_fileName = nullptr, *m_fileMeta = nullptr;
    QString m_path;
    QPushButton *m_simulate = nullptr;
    bool m_simulating = false;

    ResultUi m_cam, m_up, m_log;
};

class QStackedWidget;
class GeneratePage;
class DecodePage;
class CameraScanner;
class QTimer;

class MainWindow : public QMainWindow {
    Q_OBJECT
public:
    explicit MainWindow(QWidget *parent = nullptr);
    GeneratePage *generatePage() const { return m_generate; }
    DecodePage   *decodePage()   const { return m_decode; }

    ~MainWindow() override;

private:
    void startCamera(int index);
    void stopCamera();
    void pumpCamera();
    QWidget *buildSidebar();
    QWidget *buildTopBar();

    QStackedWidget *m_pages;
    QButtonGroup *m_nav;
    GeneratePage *m_generate;
    DecodePage *m_decode;
    CameraScanner *m_scanner = nullptr;
    QTimer *m_camTimer = nullptr;
    int m_failedReads = 0;
};

QFont   monoFont(int px, bool bold = false);

// ======================================================= mock barcode drawing

class MiniBarcode : public QWidget {                // small barcode glyph (thumbnails, payload previews)
    Q_OBJECT
public:
    explicit MiniBarcode(const QString &code, QSize size, QWidget *parent = nullptr);
    void setCode(const QString &c) { m_code = c; update(); }
protected:
    void paintEvent(QPaintEvent *) override;
private:
    QString m_code;
};

// ======================================================= Generate page: print stage
class BarcodeView : public QWidget {
    Q_OBJECT
public:
    explicit BarcodeView(QWidget *parent = nullptr);
    void setCode(const QString &code);              // placeholder bars until a real image is supplied
    void setImage(const QPixmap &pm);               // call this with the real generated barcode
    void setShowDigits(bool on);
    void setAwaiting(bool on);                      // "Awaiting State" view
    void clear();
    bool hasBarcode() const { return !m_code.isEmpty() || !m_image.isNull(); }
    QPixmap renderBarcode() const;                  // hi-res pixmap for Save / Copy
    QSize sizeHint() const override { return {560, 340}; }
protected:
    void paintEvent(QPaintEvent *) override;
private:
    void paintBarcodeCard(QPainter &p, const QRect &r) const;
    QString m_code;
    QPixmap m_image;
    bool m_digits = true, m_awaiting = false;
};

// ======================================================= Decode page: camera viewport
class CameraView : public QWidget {
    Q_OBJECT
public:
    explicit CameraView(QWidget *parent = nullptr);
    void setRunning(bool on);                       // camera on/off (off clears the picture)
    void setFrozen(bool on);                        // keep the camera running but stop updating the picture
    bool frozen() const { return m_frozen; }
    void setFrame(const QPixmap &pm);               // live camera frame (ignored while frozen)
    QSize sizeHint() const override { return {620, 350}; }
protected:
    void paintEvent(QPaintEvent *) override;
private:
    bool m_running = false, m_frozen = false;
    qreal m_phase = 0;
    QPixmap m_frame;
    QTimer m_timer;
};

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
FILE: /Frontend/src/decodepage.cpp

#include "decodepage.h"
#include "icons.h"
#include "widgets.h"

#include <QApplication>
#include <QClipboard>
#include <QComboBox>
#include <QFileInfo>
#include <QGridLayout> // used by the stat/tile grids
#include <QPainter>
#include <QProgressBar>
#include <QRegularExpression>
#include <QStackedWidget>
#include <QTime>
#include <QTimer>

// ------------------------------------------------------------------ local helpers
static QLabel *bigCode(const QString &text, int px, const QString &color)
{
    auto *l = new QLabel(text);
    QFont f = monoFont(px, true);
    f.setLetterSpacing(QFont::AbsoluteSpacing, px / 10.0);
    l->setFont(f);
    l->setStyleSheet("color:" + color + ";");
    l->setAlignment(Qt::AlignCenter);
    return l;
}

static QHBoxLayout *kv(const QString &k, QWidget *v, int px = 14)
{
    auto *h = hrow(8);
    h->addWidget(T(k, px, 400, "#434655"));
    h->addStretch();
    h->addWidget(v);
    return h;
}

// Project rule: exactly six digits, first digit must not be zero.
static bool isValidCode(const QString &s)
{
    static const QRegularExpression re("^[1-9][0-9]{5}$");
    return re.match(s).hasMatch();
}

static void wireCopy(QPushButton *b, QLabel *code)
{
    QObject::connect(b, &QPushButton::clicked, b, [b, code]
                     {
        if (b->property("copied").toBool()) return;     // double-click guard
        QApplication::clipboard()->setText(code->text());
        const QString old = b->text();
        b->setProperty("copied", true);
        b->setText(" Copied!");
        QTimer::singleShot(1300, b, [b, old] {
            b->setText(old);
            b->setProperty("copied", false);
        }); });
}

static QFrame *resultHeader(const QString &title, QLabel *badge, int px = 18)
{
    auto *f = new QFrame;
    auto *h = hbox(f, 0, 0, 0, 0, 10);
    h->addWidget(iconLabel("check", QColor("#0B4BCB"), px >= 18 ? 22 : 20));
    h->addWidget(T(title, px, 600, "#0B1C30"));
    h->addStretch();
    h->addWidget(badge);
    return f;
}

static QPixmap miniBarPixmap(const QString &code, QSize s)
{
    QPixmap pm(s);
    pm.fill(Qt::white);
    QPainter p(&pm);
    drawMockBars(p, QRect(8, 8, s.width() - 16, s.height() - 16), code, QColor("#0B1C30"));
    return pm;
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------------ construction
DecodePage::DecodePage(QWidget *parent) : QWidget(parent)
{
    setObjectName("page");
    auto *l = vbox(this, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0);
    m_stack = new QStackedWidget;
    m_stack->addWidget(buildCameraPage());
    m_stack->addWidget(buildUploadPage());
    m_stack->addWidget(buildLogsPage());
    l->addWidget(m_stack);
}

void DecodePage::setTab(int i)
{
    if (i != 0) stopCamera();                        // never keep the webcam on while another tab is shown
    m_stack->setCurrentIndex(i);
}

SegBar *DecodePage::makeTabs(int current)
{
    auto *bar = new SegBar({tr("Scan with Camera"), tr("Upload Image"), tr("Scan Logs")},
                           {"camera", "upload", "history"}, current);
    connect(bar, &SegBar::currentChanged, this, &DecodePage::setTab);
    return bar;
}

QFrame *DecodePage::makeFailBox()
{
    auto *f = panel("fail");
    auto *v = vbox(f, 16, 14, 16, 14, 8);
    auto *top = hrow();
    top->addWidget(iconChip("warn", tr("Could not read barcode"), "#FFDAD6", "#93000A", QString(), false, 12, 6));
    top->addStretch();
    top->addWidget(T("Telemetry Exception", 11, 400, "#737686", true));
    v->addLayout(top);
    auto *t = T(tr("Could not decode the barcode."), 14, 400, "#0B1C30");
    t->setObjectName("failReason");
    t->setWordWrap(true);
    v->addWidget(t);
    auto *b = hrow();
    b->addWidget(T(tr("Troubleshoot Optical Quality Guide  \xE2\x86\x92"), 12, 700, "#BA1A1A"));
    b->addStretch();
    auto *errLbl = T("ERR_UNKNOWN", 10, 400, "#737686", true);
    errLbl->setObjectName("failCode");
    b->addWidget(errLbl);
    v->addLayout(b);
    return f;
}

// ================================================================== screen 2 – camera
QWidget *DecodePage::buildCameraPage()
{
    auto *page = new QWidget;
    page->setObjectName("page");
    auto *root = vbox(page, 24, 22, 24, 24, 18);

    auto *head = hrow(14);
    head->addWidget(iconTile("scan", QColor("#0B4BCB"), 48, 24), 0, Qt::AlignTop);
    auto *tt = vcol(4);
    auto *tr1 = hrow(12);
    tr1->addWidget(T(tr("Decode Barcode"), 26, 600, "#0B1C30"));
    tr1->addWidget(chip("Increment 1", "blue"));
    tr1->addStretch();
    tt->addLayout(tr1);
    tt->addWidget(T(tr("Real-time hardware optical recognition & industrial symbology parsing"), 14, 400, "#434655"));
    head->addLayout(tt);
    head->addStretch();
    head->addWidget(monoPill("<span style='color:#0B4BCB'>\xE2\x97\x8F</span>&nbsp; ENGINE: <b>Idle</b>"), 0, Qt::AlignTop);
    root->addLayout(head);
    root->addWidget(makeTabs(0), 0, Qt::AlignLeft);

    auto *body = hrow(20);

    // ---- left card
    auto *left = card();
    auto *ll = vbox(left, 20, 20, 20, 20, 14);

    auto *bar = panel();
    auto *bl = hbox(bar, 14, 10, 14, 10, 10);
    bl->addWidget(T("CAPTURE INPUT:", 11, 500, "#737686", true));
    m_cameraBox = new QComboBox;
    m_cameraBox->addItems({tr("Camera 0 (default)"), tr("Camera 1"), tr("Camera 2")});
    m_cameraBox->setSizeAdjustPolicy(QComboBox::AdjustToMinimumContentsLengthWithIcon);
    m_cameraBox->setMinimumContentsLength(10);
    m_cameraBox->setSizePolicy(QSizePolicy::Expanding, QSizePolicy::Fixed);
    bl->addWidget(m_cameraBox, 1);
    m_stream = btn(tr("Start Camera"), "primary", "camera");
    m_freeze = btn(tr("Freeze"), "outline", "pause");
    auto *cfg = btn("", "square", "sliders");
    bl->addWidget(m_stream);
    bl->addWidget(m_freeze);
    bl->addWidget(cfg);
    ll->addWidget(bar);

    m_camera = new CameraView;
    ll->addWidget(m_camera, 1);

    auto *hint = panel();
    auto *hl = hbox(hint, 16, 12, 16, 12, 12);
    hl->addWidget(iconLabel("scan", QColor("#0B4BCB"), 20));
    m_camHint = T(tr("Press \"Start Camera\" to open the selected webcam."), 14, 400, "#1F2A44");
    m_camHint->setWordWrap(true);
    hl->addWidget(m_camHint, 1);
    hl->addWidget(iconLabel("target", QColor("#737686"), 18));
    hl->addWidget(T("Tap to\nRefocus", 11, 400, "#737686", true));
    ll->addWidget(hint);
    body->addWidget(left, 1, Qt::AlignTop);

    // ---- right column
    auto *right = vcol(16);
    auto *rc = card();
    rc->setFixedWidth(320);
    auto *rl = vbox(rc, 20, 20, 20, 20, 14);

    m_cam.detectedWording = true;
    m_cam.okKind = "green";
    m_cam.badge = pill("\xE2\x97\x8F Barcode Detected", "green");
    m_cam.badge->setContentsMargins(7, 3, 7, 3);
    {
        QFont bf = m_cam.badge->font();
        bf.setPixelSize(11);
        m_cam.badge->setFont(bf);
    }
    rl->addWidget(resultHeader(tr("Decoded Code"), m_cam.badge, 16));
    rl->addWidget(hline());

    auto *pay = panel("panelB");
    auto *pv = vbox(pay, 14, 18, 14, 16, 8);
    auto *cap = T("PAYLOAD CONTENT", 11, 600, "#737686", true);
    cap->setAlignment(Qt::AlignCenter);
    pv->addWidget(cap);
    m_cam.code = bigCode("492817", 40, "#0B1C30");
    pv->addWidget(m_cam.code);
    auto *ck = hrow(6);
    ck->addStretch();
    ck->addWidget(iconLabel("check", QColor("#0B7A4B"), 14));
    ck->addWidget(T("Modulo-103 Checksum Passed", 11, 400, "#0B7A4B", true));
    ck->addStretch();
    pv->addLayout(ck);
    rl->addWidget(pay);

    m_cam.copy = btn(tr("Copy Code"), "deep", "copy");
    m_cam.copy->setMinimumHeight(46);
    wireCopy(m_cam.copy, m_cam.code);
    rl->addWidget(m_cam.copy);
    rl->addWidget(hline());

    rl->addLayout(kv("Symbology", chip("Code 128 (Auto B)", "blue")));
    rl->addLayout(kv("Payload Length", T("6 Digits (Numeric)", 12, 400, "#0B1C30", true)));
    rl->addLayout(kv("Bounding Dimensions", T("320 \xC3\x97 94 px", 12, 400, "#0B1C30", true)));
    rl->addLayout(kv("Scan Timestamp", T("07:25:44.536 UTC", 12, 400, "#0B1C30", true)));
    rl->addLayout(kv("Decode Latency", T("14.2 ms", 12, 600, "#0B7A4B", true)));

    auto *save = btn(tr("Save to Scan History"), "tint", "history");
    save->setMinimumHeight(44);
    rl->addWidget(save);
    auto *two = hrow(10);
    auto *re = btn(tr("Re-encode"), "soft", "barcode");
    auto *api = btn(tr("Dispatch API"), "soft", "arrow");
    for (auto *b : {re, api})
        b->setStyleSheet("font-size:13px; padding:0 8px;");
    two->addWidget(re, 1);
    two->addWidget(api, 1);
    rl->addLayout(two);
    right->addWidget(rc);

    auto *gs = card();
    gs->setFixedWidth(320);
    auto *gl = hbox(gs, 16, 14, 16, 14, 12);
    gl->addWidget(iconTile("shield", QColor("#0B4BCB"), 42, 22));
    auto *gt = vcol(1);
    gt->addWidget(T("GS1 Compliance Check", 14, 600, "#0B1C30"));
    gt->addWidget(T("Standard ISO/IEC 15417 Verified", 13, 400, "#434655"));
    gl->addLayout(gt, 1);
    right->addWidget(gs);
    right->addStretch();
    body->addLayout(right);
    root->addLayout(body, 1);

    // ---- wiring
    m_freeze->setEnabled(false);
    connect(m_stream, &QPushButton::clicked, this, [this] {
        if (m_streaming) { stopCamera(); return; }
        setCameraActive(true);                       // optimistic; MainWindow reverts it if the camera can't open
        setCameraMessage(tr("Opening camera..."));
        emit cameraStartRequested(m_cameraBox->currentIndex());
    });
    connect(m_freeze, &QPushButton::clicked, this, [this] {
        const bool frozen = !m_camera->frozen();
        m_camera->setFrozen(frozen);
        m_freeze->setText(frozen ? " Resume" : " Freeze"); });
    return page;
}

QWidget *DecodePage::buildUploadPage()
{
    auto *page = new QWidget;

    dl->addWidget(m_simulate);
    right->addWidget(dg);
    right->addStretch();
    body->addLayout(right);
    root->addLayout(body, 1);

    // ---- wiring
    connect(drop, &DropZone::fileChosen, this, &DecodePage::loadImage);
    connect(remove, &QPushButton::clicked, this, [this]
            { m_loaded->hide(); m_path.clear(); resetResult(); });
    connect(decode, &QPushButton::clicked, this, [this]
            {
        if (m_path.isEmpty()) {
            showFailure(tr("No image loaded. Please upload an image first."), "ERR_NO_IMAGE");

        emit decodeImageRequested(m_path); });

        showResult("749205"); });
    connect(s2, &QPushButton::clicked, this, [this]
            {
        m_loaded->show(); m_path.clear();
        m_fileName->setText("sample_damaged_scan.png");
        m_thumb->setPixmap(miniBarPixmap("000000", QSize(48, 40)));
        showFailure(); });
    connect(m_simulate, &QPushButton::clicked, this, [this]
            {
        m_simulating = !m_simulating;
        if (m_simulating) { showFailure(); m_simulate->setText(tr("Restore Success")); }
        else { showResult("749205"); m_simulate->setText(tr("Simulate Error")); } });
    return page;
}

void DecodePage::loadImage(const QString &path)
{
    const QFileInfo fi(path);
    if (!fi.exists() || !fi.isFile())
    {
        m_path.clear();
        m_loaded->hide();
        showFailure(tr("File not found."), "ERR_FILE_NOT_FOUND");
        return;
    }
    if (fi.size() > 32LL * 1024 * 1024)
    {
        m_path.clear();
        m_loaded->hide();
        showFailure(tr("File exceeds the 32 MB limit."), "ERR_FILE_TOO_LARGE");
        return;
    }
    QPixmap pm(path);
    if (pm.isNull())
    {
        m_path.clear();
        m_loaded->hide();
        showFailure(tr("Unsupported or corrupted image."), "ERR_BAD_IMAGE");
        return;
    }
    m_path = path;
    m_thumb->setPixmap(pm.scaled(QSize(54, 44), Qt::KeepAspectRatio, Qt::SmoothTransformation));
    m_fileName->setText(fi.fileName());
    m_fileMeta->setText(QString("%1 KB &nbsp;\xE2\x80\xA2&nbsp; <span style='color:#0B4BCB'>%2 \xC3\x97 %3 px</span>")
                            .arg(qMax<qint64>(1, fi.size() / 1024))
                            .arg(pm.width())
                            .arg(pm.height()));
    m_loaded->show();
    resetResult();
}

// ================================================================== result state (all screens)
void DecodePage::applyResult(const ResultUi &r, const QString &code)
{
    if (!r.code)
        return;
    r.code->setText(code);
    r.code->setStyleSheet("color:#0B1C30;");
    if (r.badge)
        setBadge(r.badge, r.detectedWording ? "\xE2\x97\x8F Barcode Detected" : "\xE2\x97\x8F  Success", r.okKind);
    if (r.copy)
        r.copy->setEnabled(true);
    if (r.fail)
        r.fail->hide();
}

void DecodePage::showResult(const QString &code)
{
    if (!isValidCode(code))
    {
        showFailure(tr("Invalid code: must be exactly 6 digits, first digit non-zero."), "ERR_INVALID_CODE");
        return;
    }
    applyResult(m_cam, code);
    applyResult(m_up, code);
    m_log.code->setText(code); // overview keeps its permanent telemetry box
    m_log.copy->setEnabled(true);
    if (m_log.fail) m_log.fail->hide();
}

void DecodePage::showFailure(const QString &reason, const QString &errCode)
{
    for (const ResultUi *r : {&m_cam, &m_up})
    {
        if (!r->code)
            continue;
        r->code->setText("------");
        r->code->setStyleSheet("color:#C2CCDF;");
        if (r->badge)
            setBadge(r->badge, "Could not read barcode", "red");
        if (r->copy)
            r->copy->setEnabled(false);
    }
    for (QFrame *f : {m_up.fail, m_log.fail})
    {
        if (!f)
            continue;
        if (auto *t = f->findChild<QLabel *>("failReason"))
            t->setText(reason.isEmpty() ? tr("Could not decode the barcode.") : reason);
        if (auto *c = f->findChild<QLabel *>("failCode"))
            c->setText(errCode.isEmpty() ? QString("ERR_UNKNOWN") : errCode);
        f->show();
    }
}

void DecodePage::resetResult()
{
    for (const ResultUi *r : {&m_cam, &m_up})
    {
        if (!r->code)
            continue;
        r->code->setText("------");
        r->code->setStyleSheet("color:#C2CCDF;");
        if (r->badge)
            setBadge(r->badge, "\xE2\x97\x8F  Waiting", "idle");
        if (r->copy)
            r->copy->setEnabled(false);
        if (r->fail)
            r->fail->hide();
    }
    if (m_log.fail) m_log.fail->hide();
}

void DecodePage::setCameraActive(bool on)
{
    m_streaming = on;
    m_camera->setRunning(on);
    m_stream->setText(on ? " Stop Camera" : " Start Camera");
    setKind(m_stream, on ? "deep" : "primary");
    m_cameraBox->setEnabled(!on);
    m_freeze->setEnabled(on);
    m_freeze->setText(" Freeze");
    if (!on) setCameraMessage(tr("Press \"Start Camera\" to open the selected webcam."));
    else     setCameraMessage(tr("Hold the barcode steady inside the frame."));
}

void DecodePage::setCameraMessage(const QString &text, bool error)
{
    m_camHint->setText(text);
    m_camHint->setStyleSheet(QString("font-size:14px; color:%1;").arg(error ? "#BA1A1A" : "#1F2A44"));
}

void DecodePage::stopCamera()
{
    if (!m_streaming) return;
    setCameraActive(false);
    emit cameraStopRequested();
}

void DecodePage::setCameraList(const QStringList &names)
{
    m_cameraBox->clear();
    m_cameraBox->addItems(names.isEmpty() ? QStringList{tr("No camera found")} : names);
}

#include "generatepage.h"
#include "icons.h"
#include "widgets.h"

// --------------------------------------------------- local helpers
// Project rule: exactly six digits, first digit must not be zero.
static bool isValidCode(const QString &s)
{
    static const QRegularExpression re("^[1-9][0-9]{5}$");
    return re.match(s).hasMatch();
}

static void showInputError(QLineEdit *input, QLabel *hint, QLabel *icon, const QString &msg)
{
    setKind(input, "error");
    hint->setText(msg);
    hint->setStyleSheet("font-size:13px; color:#BA1A1A;");
    icon->setPixmap(iconPixmap("warn", QColor("#BA1A1A"), 16));
}

void GeneratePage::setBarcodeImage(const QPixmap &pm) { m_view->setImage(pm); }

void GeneratePage::setCode(const QString &code, bool generate)
{
    if (code.isEmpty())
    {
        m_input->clear();
        return;
    }
    if (!generate)
    {
        m_input->setText(code);
        return;
    } // prefill only; onTextChanged() shows any problem

    if (!isValidCode(code))
    {
        m_input->clear();                           // resets hint state first ...
        showInputError(m_input, m_hint, m_hintIcon, // ... then shows the error
                       tr("Invalid code: must be exactly 6 digits, first digit not zero"));
        return;
    }
    m_input->setText(code);
    m_view->setCode(code);
    m_stageSeg->setCurrent(0);
    m_view->setAwaiting(false);
    setProperty("genCode", code); // the code that is actually shown in the preview
    m_save->setEnabled(true);
    m_copy->setEnabled(true);
}

void GeneratePage::onTextChanged(const QString &t)
{
    m_counter->setText(QString("%1 / 6").arg(t.size()));
    const bool valid = isValidCode(t);
    m_generate->setEnabled(valid);
    m_validChip->setVisible(valid);
    m_checkAction->setVisible(valid);

    setKind(m_input, "");
    m_hint->setText(tr("Must be exactly 6 digits, first digit not zero"));
    m_hint->setStyleSheet("font-size:13px; color:#434655;");
    m_hintIcon->setPixmap(iconPixmap("info", QColor("#0B4BCB"), 16));

    if (!t.isEmpty() && t.at(0) == QLatin1Char('0'))
        showInputError(m_input, m_hint, m_hintIcon, tr("First digit must not be zero"));
}

void GeneratePage::onGenerate()
{
    const QString code = m_input->text();
    if (!isValidCode(code))
    { // defensive: button/Enter should already be blocked
        m_generate->setEnabled(false);
        showInputError(m_input, m_hint, m_hintIcon,
                       tr("Invalid code: must be exactly 6 digits, first digit not zero"));
        return;
    }

    m_view->setCode(code);
    m_stageSeg->setCurrent(0);
    m_view->setAwaiting(false);
    setProperty("genCode", code);
    m_save->setEnabled(true);
    m_copy->setEnabled(true);

    for (int i = m_sessions.size() - 1; i >= 0; --i)
        if (m_sessions[i].code == code)
            m_sessions.removeAt(i);
    m_sessions.prepend({code, "Code 128", QTime::currentTime().toString("HH:mm:ss")});
    while (m_sessions.size() > 3)
        m_sessions.removeLast();
    rebuildSessions();
    emit generateRequested(code);
}

void GeneratePage::onClear()
{
    m_input->clear();
    m_view->clear();
    setProperty("genCode", QString());
    m_save->setEnabled(false);
    m_copy->setEnabled(false);
    m_input->setFocus();
}

void GeneratePage::onSave()
{
    const QString code = property("genCode").toString();
    if (code.isEmpty())
    {
        QMessageBox::warning(this, tr("Save barcode"), tr("Generate a barcode first."));
        return;
    }
    const QPixmap pm = m_view->renderBarcode();
    if (pm.isNull())
    {
        QMessageBox::warning(this, tr("Save barcode"), tr("Could not render the barcode image."));
        return;
    }
    QString path = QFileDialog::getSaveFileName(this, tr("Save barcode"), code + ".png", tr("PNG image (*.png)"));
    if (path.isEmpty())
        return; // user cancelled
    if (!path.endsWith(".png", Qt::CaseInsensitive))
        path += ".png";
    if (!pm.save(path, "PNG"))
        QMessageBox::warning(this, tr("Save barcode"),
                             tr("Could not save the file:\n%1\n\nCheck the folder permissions and free disk space.").arg(path));
}

void GeneratePage::onCopy()
{
    if (m_copy->property("copied").toBool())
        return; // double-click guard
    const QPixmap pm = m_view->renderBarcode();
    if (pm.isNull())
    {
        QMessageBox::warning(this, tr("Copy image"), tr("Could not render the barcode image."));
        return;
    }
    QApplication::clipboard()->setPixmap(pm);
    const QString old = m_copy->text();
    m_copy->setProperty("copied", true);
    m_copy->setText(tr(" Copied!"));
    QTimer::singleShot(1500, this, [this, old]
                       {
        m_copy->setText(old);
        m_copy->setProperty("copied", false); });
}

void GeneratePage::rebuildSessions()
{
    while (m_sessionRow->count())
    {
        QLayoutItem *it = m_sessionRow->takeAt(0);
        if (it->widget())
            it->widget()->deleteLater();
        delete it;
    }
    if (m_sessions.isEmpty())
    {
        m_sessionRow->addWidget(T(tr("No barcodes generated yet"), 12, 400, "#737686", true), 1);
        return;
    }
    for (int i = 0; i < m_sessions.size(); ++i)
    {
        const Session &s = m_sessions[i];
        auto *b = new QPushButton;
        b->setObjectName("tile");
        b->setProperty("active", i == 0);
        b->setCursor(Qt::PointingHandCursor);
        b->setMinimumHeight(70);
        auto *l = vbox(b, 14, 10, 14, 10, 4);
        auto *top = hrow();
        auto *code = T(s.code, 16, 600, i == 0 ? "#0B4BCB" : "#0B1C30", true);
        top->addWidget(code);
        top->addStretch();
        top->addWidget(chip(i == 0 ? "Active" : "Logged", i == 0 ? "blue" : "neutral"));
        l->addLayout(top);
        l->addWidget(T(s.symbology + " \xE2\x80\xA2 " + s.time, 11, 400, "#737686", true));
        for (auto *w : b->findChildren<QWidget *>())
            w->setAttribute(Qt::WA_TransparentForMouseEvents);
        connect(b, &QPushButton::clicked, this, [this, c = s.code]
                { setCode(c, true); });
        m_sessionRow->addWidget(b, 1);
    }
}


--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
FILE: /Frontend/src/icons.cpp
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
#include "icons.h"

#include <QPainter>
#include <QPainterPath>
#include <QPolygonF>

QPixmap iconPixmap(const QString &n, const QColor &c, int size)
{
    const qreal dpr = 2.0;
    QPixmap pm(int(size * dpr), int(size * dpr));
    pm.setDevicePixelRatio(dpr);
    pm.fill(Qt::transparent);

    QPainter p(&pm);
    p.setRenderHint(QPainter::Antialiasing);
    p.scale(size / 20.0, size / 20.0);
    QPen pen(c, 1.7, Qt::SolidLine, Qt::RoundCap, Qt::RoundJoin);
    p.setPen(pen);
    p.setBrush(Qt::NoBrush);
    auto dot = [&](qreal x, qreal y, qreal r) {
        p.setPen(Qt::NoPen); p.setBrush(c); p.drawEllipse(QPointF(x, y), r, r);
        p.setPen(pen); p.setBrush(Qt::NoBrush);
    };

    if (n == "barcode") {
        p.setPen(Qt::NoPen); p.setBrush(c);
        const qreal xs[] = {2.5, 5, 7.2, 9.5, 12.4, 14.4, 16.6};
        const qreal ws[] = {1.4, 1.0, 1.8, 1.0, 1.4, 1.0, 1.0};
        for (int i = 0; i < 7; ++i) p.drawRect(QRectF(xs[i], 3.5, ws[i], 13));
    } else if (n == "scan") {
        p.drawLine(QPointF(3, 7.5), QPointF(3, 3)); p.drawLine(QPointF(3, 3), QPointF(7.5, 3));
        p.drawLine(QPointF(12.5, 3), QPointF(17, 3)); p.drawLine(QPointF(17, 3), QPointF(17, 7.5));
        p.drawLine(QPointF(3, 12.5), QPointF(3, 17)); p.drawLine(QPointF(3, 17), QPointF(7.5, 17));
        p.drawLine(QPointF(12.5, 17), QPointF(17, 17)); p.drawLine(QPointF(17, 17), QPointF(17, 12.5));
        p.drawLine(QPointF(6, 10), QPointF(14, 10));
    } else if (n == "camera") {
        p.drawRoundedRect(QRectF(2.5, 5.5, 10.5, 9), 2, 2);
        QPolygonF t; t << QPointF(13.5, 8.8) << QPointF(17.5, 6.3) << QPointF(17.5, 13.7) << QPointF(13.5, 11.2);
        p.drawPolygon(t);
    } else if (n == "upload" || n == "download") {
        const bool up = (n == "upload");
        const qreal a = up ? 3.5 : 12.5, b = up ? 12.5 : 3.5;
        p.drawLine(QPointF(10, a), QPointF(10, b + (up ? 0 : 0)));
        if (up) { p.drawLine(QPointF(6.5, 7), QPointF(10, 3.5)); p.drawLine(QPointF(13.5, 7), QPointF(10, 3.5)); }
        else    { p.drawLine(QPointF(6.5, 9), QPointF(10, 12.5)); p.drawLine(QPointF(13.5, 9), QPointF(10, 12.5)); }
        p.drawLine(QPointF(3.5, 13), QPointF(3.5, 16.5)); p.drawLine(QPointF(3.5, 16.5), QPointF(16.5, 16.5));
        p.drawLine(QPointF(16.5, 16.5), QPointF(16.5, 13));
    } else if (n == "copy") {
        p.drawRoundedRect(QRectF(7.5, 7.5, 9, 9), 2, 2);
        QPainterPath b; b.moveTo(4.5, 12.5); b.lineTo(4.5, 5.5); b.quadTo(4.5, 3.5, 6.5, 3.5); b.lineTo(12.5, 3.5);
        p.drawPath(b);
    } else if (n == "check") {
        p.drawEllipse(QRectF(2.5, 2.5, 15, 15));
        QPainterPath t; t.moveTo(6.4, 10.2); t.lineTo(9, 12.8); t.lineTo(13.7, 7.6); p.drawPath(t);
    } else if (n == "info") {
        p.drawEllipse(QRectF(2.5, 2.5, 15, 15));
        p.drawLine(QPointF(10, 9), QPointF(10, 13.8)); dot(10, 6.5, 0.9);
    } else if (n == "trash") {
        p.drawLine(QPointF(3.5, 6), QPointF(16.5, 6));
        QPainterPath h; h.moveTo(7.8, 6); h.lineTo(7.8, 3.8); h.lineTo(12.2, 3.8); h.lineTo(12.2, 6); p.drawPath(h);
        QPainterPath b; b.moveTo(5, 6); b.lineTo(5.9, 16.2); b.lineTo(14.1, 16.2); b.lineTo(15, 6); p.drawPath(b);
        p.drawLine(QPointF(8.5, 9), QPointF(8.5, 13.5)); p.drawLine(QPointF(11.5, 9), QPointF(11.5, 13.5));
    } else if (n == "shield") {
        QPainterPath s; s.moveTo(10, 2.5); s.lineTo(16.5, 5); s.lineTo(16.5, 10);
        s.cubicTo(16.5, 14, 13.5, 16.5, 10, 17.5); s.cubicTo(6.5, 16.5, 3.5, 14, 3.5, 10); s.lineTo(3.5, 5); s.closeSubpath();
        p.drawPath(s);
        QPainterPath t; t.moveTo(7, 10); t.lineTo(9.2, 12.2); t.lineTo(13, 8); p.drawPath(t);
    } else if (n == "user") {
        p.drawEllipse(QRectF(7, 3.5, 6, 6));
        QPainterPath b; b.moveTo(4, 17); b.cubicTo(4, 13.5, 7, 12, 10, 12); b.cubicTo(13, 12, 16, 13.5, 16, 17); p.drawPath(b);
    } else if (n == "history") {
        p.drawEllipse(QRectF(3, 3, 14, 14));
        QPainterPath h; h.moveTo(10, 6.2); h.lineTo(10, 10); h.lineTo(12.8, 11.8); p.drawPath(h);
    } else if (n == "print") {
        p.drawRect(QRectF(5.5, 3, 9, 4.5));
        p.drawRoundedRect(QRectF(3, 7.5, 14, 6), 1.5, 1.5);
        p.drawRect(QRectF(5.5, 11.5, 9, 5.5));
    } else if (n == "hash") {
        p.drawLine(QPointF(8, 3.5), QPointF(6.5, 16.5)); p.drawLine(QPointF(13.5, 3.5), QPointF(12, 16.5));
        p.drawLine(QPointF(4, 7.5), QPointF(16.5, 7.5)); p.drawLine(QPointF(3.5, 12.5), QPointF(16, 12.5));
    } else if (n == "braces") {
        QFont f; f.setPixelSize(14); f.setBold(true); p.setFont(f);
        p.drawText(QRectF(0, 0, 20, 19), Qt::AlignCenter, "{ }");
    } else if (n == "server") {
        p.drawRoundedRect(QRectF(3, 3.5, 14, 5.2), 1.6, 1.6);
        p.drawRoundedRect(QRectF(3, 11.3, 14, 5.2), 1.6, 1.6);
        dot(6.2, 6.1, 0.9); dot(6.2, 13.9, 0.9);
    } else if (n == "sliders") {
        p.drawLine(QPointF(3, 6), QPointF(17, 6)); p.drawLine(QPointF(3, 14), QPointF(17, 14));
        p.setBrush(Qt::white); p.drawEllipse(QPointF(7, 6), 2.2, 2.2); p.drawEllipse(QPointF(13, 14), 2.2, 2.2);
    } else if (n == "pause") {
        p.drawEllipse(QRectF(2.5, 2.5, 15, 15));
        p.drawLine(QPointF(8, 7), QPointF(8, 13)); p.drawLine(QPointF(12, 7), QPointF(12, 13));
    } else if (n == "bolt") {
        QPolygonF b; b << QPointF(11, 2.5) << QPointF(5, 11) << QPointF(9.5, 11) << QPointF(8.6, 17.5)
                       << QPointF(15, 8.5) << QPointF(10.5, 8.5);
        p.drawPolygon(b);
    } else if (n == "warn") {
        QPolygonF t; t << QPointF(10, 3) << QPointF(17.5, 16.3) << QPointF(2.5, 16.3);
        p.drawPolygon(t);
        p.drawLine(QPointF(10, 8), QPointF(10, 12)); dot(10, 14.2, 0.8);
    } else if (n == "image") {
        p.drawRoundedRect(QRectF(3, 4, 14, 12), 2, 2); dot(7.2, 8, 1.2);
        QPainterPath m; m.moveTo(4, 14); m.lineTo(8, 10.6); m.lineTo(11, 13); m.lineTo(13.5, 11); m.lineTo(16, 14); p.drawPath(m);
    } else if (n == "target") {
        p.drawEllipse(QRectF(3.5, 3.5, 13, 13)); dot(10, 10, 1.7);
        p.drawLine(QPointF(10, 1.5), QPointF(10, 4)); p.drawLine(QPointF(10, 16), QPointF(10, 18.5));
        p.drawLine(QPointF(1.5, 10), QPointF(4, 10)); p.drawLine(QPointF(16, 10), QPointF(18.5, 10));
    } else if (n == "ruler") {
        p.drawRoundedRect(QRectF(2.5, 6.5, 15, 7), 1.2, 1.2);
        p.drawLine(QPointF(6, 6.5), QPointF(6, 10)); p.drawLine(QPointF(10, 6.5), QPointF(10, 11)); p.drawLine(QPointF(14, 6.5), QPointF(14, 10));
    } else if (n == "backspace") {
        QPolygonF b; b << QPointF(7, 4.5) << QPointF(17, 4.5) << QPointF(17, 15.5) << QPointF(7, 15.5) << QPointF(2.5, 10);
        p.drawPolygon(b);
        p.drawLine(QPointF(10, 8), QPointF(14, 12)); p.drawLine(QPointF(14, 8), QPointF(10, 12));
    } else if (n == "folder") {
        p.drawRoundedRect(QRectF(2.5, 6.5, 15, 9.5), 2, 2);
        QPainterPath t; t.moveTo(2.5, 7); t.lineTo(2.5, 5); t.quadTo(2.5, 4, 3.5, 4); t.lineTo(7.5, 4); t.lineTo(9.2, 6.5); p.drawPath(t);
    } else if (n == "bug") {
        p.drawEllipse(QRectF(6, 6, 8, 10));
        p.drawLine(QPointF(3, 8), QPointF(6.2, 9.2)); p.drawLine(QPointF(3, 13.5), QPointF(6.2, 12.3));
        p.drawLine(QPointF(17, 8), QPointF(13.8, 9.2)); p.drawLine(QPointF(17, 13.5), QPointF(13.8, 12.3));
        p.drawLine(QPointF(8, 3.5), QPointF(9, 6)); p.drawLine(QPointF(12, 3.5), QPointF(11, 6));
    } else if (n == "gauge") {
        p.drawArc(QRectF(2.5, 4.5, 15, 15), 0, 180 * 16);
        p.drawLine(QPointF(10, 12), QPointF(13.5, 8)); p.drawLine(QPointF(2.5, 12), QPointF(17.5, 12));
    } else if (n == "arrow") {
        p.drawLine(QPointF(4, 10), QPointF(16, 10));
        p.drawLine(QPointF(11.5, 5.5), QPointF(16, 10)); p.drawLine(QPointF(11.5, 14.5), QPointF(16, 10));
    }
    return pm;
}

QIcon makeIcon(const QString &name, const QColor &color, int size)
{
    return QIcon(iconPixmap(name, color, size));
}

QLabel *iconLabel(const QString &name, const QColor &color, int size)
{
    auto *l = new QLabel;
    l->setPixmap(iconPixmap(name, color, size));
    l->setFixedSize(size, size);
    l->setStyleSheet("background: transparent; border: none;");
    return l;
}

#include <QApplication>
#include <QFont>
#include "mainwindow.h"
#include "theme.h"

int main(int argc, char *argv[])
{
    QApplication app(argc, argv);
    app.setApplicationName("BarcodeHub");
    app.setStyle("Fusion");                              // consistent base on every OS

    QFont f;
    f.setFamilies({"Inter", "Segoe UI", "Noto Sans", "Helvetica Neue", "Arial"});
    f.setPixelSize(13);
    app.setFont(f);
    app.setStyleSheet(Theme::styleSheet());

    MainWindow w;
    w.show();
    return app.exec();
}

#include "mainwindow.h"
#include "decodepage.h"
#include "generatepage.h"
#include "icons.h"
#include "widgets.h"
#include "scanner/CameraScanner.h"
#include "barcode/BarcodeGenerator.h"

#include <QPixmap>
#include <QStackedWidget>

// brand logo tile (barcode glyph on a blue gradient)

class LogoTile : public QWidget {
public:
    explicit LogoTile(int s) { setFixedSize(s, s); }
protected:
    void paintEvent(QPaintEvent *) override
    {
        QPainter p(this);
        p.setRenderHint(QPainter::Antialiasing);
        QLinearGradient g(0, 0, width(), height());
        g.setColorAt(0, QColor("#2F6BF0"));
        g.setColorAt(1, QColor("#0B3FB8"));
        p.setPen(Qt::NoPen);
        p.setBrush(g);
        p.drawRoundedRect(rect(), width() * 0.26, width() * 0.26);
        p.setBrush(Qt::white);
        const double u = width() / 32.0;
        const double xs[] = {8, 11.5, 14.5, 19, 22.5}, ws[] = {2, 1.2, 2.6, 1.2, 2};
        for (int i = 0; i < 5; ++i) p.drawRect(QRectF(xs[i] * u, 8.5 * u, ws[i] * u, 15 * u));
    }
};

static QScrollArea *scrollWrap(QWidget *page)
{
    auto *sa = new QScrollArea;
    sa->setWidgetResizable(true);
    sa->setFrameShape(QFrame::NoFrame);
    sa->setHorizontalScrollBarPolicy(Qt::ScrollBarAlwaysOff);
    sa->setWidget(page);
    return sa;
}

MainWindow::MainWindow(QWidget *parent) : QMainWindow(parent)
{
    setWindowTitle("BarcodeHub");
    resize(1360, 900);
    setMinimumSize(1180, 700);

    auto *root = new QWidget;
    root->setObjectName("root");
    setCentralWidget(root);
    auto *h = hbox(root, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0);
    h->addWidget(buildSidebar());

    auto *right = vcol(0);
    right->addWidget(buildTopBar());
    m_generate = new GeneratePage;
    m_decode = new DecodePage;
    m_pages = new QStackedWidget;
    m_pages->addWidget(scrollWrap(m_generate));
    m_pages->addWidget(scrollWrap(m_decode));
    right->addWidget(m_pages, 1);
    h->addLayout(right, 1);
   
    connect(m_generate, &GeneratePage::generateRequested, this, [this](const QString &code) {
    BarcodeGenerator generator;
    BarcodeResult result = generator.generate(code);

    if (result.success) {
        m_generate->setBarcodeImage(QPixmap::fromImage(result.image));
    }
});

    connect(m_decode, &DecodePage::generateFromCodeRequested, this, [this](const QString &code) {
        m_generate->setCode(code, true);
        m_nav->button(0)->click();
    });

    // ---- live camera: Backend CameraScanner -> CameraView in the Decode > Camera tab

    m_scanner = new CameraScanner;
    m_camTimer = new QTimer(this);
    m_camTimer->setInterval(33);                     // ~30 fps; reads happen on the GUI thread
    connect(m_camTimer, &QTimer::timeout, this, &MainWindow::pumpCamera);
    connect(m_decode, &DecodePage::cameraStartRequested, this, &MainWindow::startCamera);
    connect(m_decode, &DecodePage::cameraStopRequested, this, &MainWindow::stopCamera);

  
    // PLACEHOLDER (UI demo only) – delete once the real decoder exists.
    // Pressing "Decode Image" just alternates the success / failure states.
   
    connect(m_decode, &DecodePage::decodeImageRequested, this, [this](const QString &) {
        static bool ok = false;
        if (ok) m_decode->showResult("749205"); else m_decode->showFailure();
        ok = !ok;
    });
}

MainWindow::~MainWindow()
{

    // release the webcam before the window goes away
    stopCamera();                                  
    delete m_scanner;
}

void MainWindow::startCamera(int index)
{
#ifndef HAVE_OPENCV
    m_decode->setCameraActive(false);
    m_decode->setCameraMessage(tr("This build has no OpenCV, so the webcam cannot be used. "
                                  "Install OpenCV and reconfigure CMake."), true);
    return;
#else
    QString error;
    m_failedReads = 0;
    if (!m_scanner->openCamera(index, &error)) {
        m_decode->setCameraActive(false);
        m_decode->setCameraMessage(error.isEmpty() ? tr("Could not open camera %1.").arg(index) : error, true);
        return;
    }
    m_camTimer->start();
#endif
}

void MainWindow::stopCamera()
{
    if (m_camTimer) m_camTimer->stop();
    if (m_scanner) m_scanner->closeCamera();
}

void MainWindow::pumpCamera()
{
    if (!m_scanner->isOpened()) { stopCamera(); return; }
    const CameraFrameResult r = m_scanner->readFrame();
    if (r.success && !r.frame.isNull()) {
        m_failedReads = 0;
        // mirrored for display only (like a mirror / selfie view); the original frame stays unflipped for decoding
        m_decode->cameraView()->setFrame(QPixmap::fromImage(r.frame.mirrored(true, false)));
        return;
    }
    if (++m_failedReads >= 30) {                     // ~1 s without a frame: camera unplugged / busy
        stopCamera();
        m_decode->setCameraActive(false);
        m_decode->setCameraMessage(r.error.isEmpty() ? tr("Camera stopped delivering frames.") : r.error, true);
    }
}

QWidget *MainWindow::buildSidebar()
{
    auto *sb = new QFrame;
    sb->setObjectName("sidebar");
    sb->setFixedWidth(240);
    auto *l = vbox(sb, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0);

    auto *brand = new QFrame;
    brand->setObjectName("brandRow");
    brand->setFixedHeight(56);
    auto *bh = hbox(brand, 16, 0, 16, 0, 10);
    bh->addWidget(new LogoTile(32));
    auto *bt = vcol(-5);
    bt->addWidget(T("BarcodeHub", 16, 600, "#0B1C30"));
    bt->addWidget(T("Precision Utility", 11, 400, "#434655"));
    bh->addLayout(bt);
    bh->addStretch();
    l->addWidget(brand);

    auto *inner = new QWidget;
    auto *il = vbox(inner, 12, 12, 12, 12, 4);
    auto *cap = T("OPERATIONS", 11, 600, "#737686");
    cap->setContentsMargins(8, 4, 0, 4);
    il->addWidget(cap);

    m_nav = new QButtonGroup(this);
    m_nav->setExclusive(true);
    const QString names[] = {tr("Generate Barcode"), tr("Decode Barcode")};
    const QString icons[] = {"barcode", "scan"};
    for (int i = 0; i < 2; ++i) {
        auto *b = new QPushButton("  " + names[i]);
        b->setObjectName("nav");
        b->setCheckable(true);
        b->setCursor(Qt::PointingHandCursor);
        QIcon ic;
        ic.addPixmap(iconPixmap(icons[i], QColor("#434655"), 20), QIcon::Normal, QIcon::Off);
        ic.addPixmap(iconPixmap(icons[i], QColor("#004AC6"), 20), QIcon::Normal, QIcon::On);
        b->setIcon(ic);
        b->setIconSize(QSize(20, 20));
        m_nav->addButton(b, i);
        il->addWidget(b);
        if (i == 0) b->setChecked(true);
    }
    connect(m_nav, &QButtonGroup::idClicked, this, [this](int id) {
        if (id != 1) m_decode->stopCamera();         // leaving Decode: switch the webcam off
        m_pages->setCurrentIndex(id);
    });
    il->addStretch();
    l->addWidget(inner, 1);

    auto *foot = new QWidget;
    auto *fl = vbox(foot, 12, 0, 12, 14, 0);
    auto *chipRow = panel();
    auto *cr = hbox(chipRow, 12, 9, 12, 9, 8);
    cr->addWidget(T("\xE2\x97\x8F", 9, 400, "#0B4BCB"));
    cr->addWidget(T("Engine v2.4", 11, 500, "#434655", true));
    cr->addStretch();
    cr->addWidget(T("1440x900", 11, 400, "#737686", true));
    fl->addWidget(chipRow);
    l->addWidget(foot);
    return sb;
}

QWidget *MainWindow::buildTopBar()
{
    auto *tb = new QFrame;
    tb->setObjectName("topbar");
    tb->setFixedHeight(56);
    auto *l = hbox(tb, 24, 0, 24, 0, 12);
    l->addWidget(new LogoTile(30), 0, Qt::AlignVCenter);
    l->addWidget(T("BarcodeHub", 17, 600, "#0B1C30"), 0, Qt::AlignVCenter);
    auto *sep = new QFrame;
    sep->setFixedSize(1, 20);
    sep->setStyleSheet("background:#DDE2EC; border:none;");
    l->addWidget(sep, 0, Qt::AlignVCenter);
    l->addWidget(pill("\xE2\x97\x8F  Increment 1", "tint"), 0, Qt::AlignVCenter);
    l->addStretch();
    l->addWidget(iconChip("server", "Hardware Link Active", "#EFF4FF", "#434655", "#E1E8F7", true, 12, 8), 0, Qt::AlignVCenter);
    l->addSpacing(8);
    auto *av = new QFrame;
    av->setObjectName("avatar");
    av->setFixedSize(40, 40);
    auto *al = hbox(av, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0);
    al->setAlignment(Qt::AlignCenter);
    al->addWidget(iconLabel("user", QColor("#FFFFFF"), 20));
    l->addWidget(av, 0, Qt::AlignVCenter);
    return tb;
}

#include "widgets.h"
