// ======================================================= helpers
QVBoxLayout *vbox(QWidget *w, int l, int t, int r, int b, int s)
{
    auto *v = new QVBoxLayout(w);
    v->setContentsMargins(l, t, r, b);
    v->setSpacing(s);
    return v;
}
QHBoxLayout *hbox(QWidget *w, int l, int t, int r, int b, int s)
{
    auto *h = new QHBoxLayout(w);
    h->setContentsMargins(l, t, r, b);
    h->setSpacing(s);
    return h;
}
QHBoxLayout *hrow(int s) { auto *h = new QHBoxLayout; h->setContentsMargins(0, 0, 0, 0); h->setSpacing(s); return h; }
QVBoxLayout *vcol(int s) { auto *v = new QVBoxLayout; v->setContentsMargins(0, 0, 0, 0); v->setSpacing(s); return v; }

QString monoFamily() { return QStringLiteral("'JetBrains Mono','Consolas','DejaVu Sans Mono',monospace"); }

QFont monoFont(int px, bool bold)
{
    QFont f;
    f.setFamilies({"JetBrains Mono", "Consolas", "DejaVu Sans Mono", "Courier New"});
    f.setStyleHint(QFont::Monospace);
    f.setPixelSize(px);
    f.setBold(bold);
    return f;
}

QLabel *T(const QString &text, int px, int weight, const QString &color, bool mono)
{
    auto *l = new QLabel(text);
    QString css = QString("font-size:%1px; font-weight:%2; color:%3;").arg(px).arg(weight).arg(color);
    if (mono) css += "font-family:" + monoFamily() + ";";
    l->setStyleSheet(css);
    return l;
}

QLabel *caption(const QString &text, bool mono) { return T(text, 11, 600, "#737686", mono); }

// Pills and chips are painted by hand: Qt stylesheets drop border-radius on QLabel when
// the colour comes from a dynamic-property rule, so custom painting is the reliable way.
class Badge : public QLabel {
public:
    Badge(const QString &text, const QString &kind, bool isPill) : QLabel(text), m_pill(isPill)
    {
        setProperty("kind", kind);
        QFont f = isPill ? QFont() : monoFont(11, true);
        f.setPixelSize(isPill ? 12 : 11);
        f.setWeight(QFont::DemiBold);
        setFont(f);
        setContentsMargins(isPill ? 11 : 8, isPill ? 3 : 2, isPill ? 11 : 8, isPill ? 3 : 2);
        restyle();
    }
protected:
    bool event(QEvent *e) override
    {
        if (e->type() == QEvent::DynamicPropertyChange) restyle();
        return QLabel::event(e);
    }
    void paintEvent(QPaintEvent *e) override
    {
        {
            QPainter p(this);
            p.setRenderHint(QPainter::Antialiasing);
            p.setBrush(m_bg);
            p.setPen(m_border.isValid() ? QPen(m_border, 1) : QPen(Qt::NoPen));
            const qreal r = m_pill ? height() / 2.0 : 6;
            p.drawRoundedRect(QRectF(rect()).adjusted(0.5, 0.5, -0.5, -0.5), r, r);
        }
        QLabel::paintEvent(e);
    }
private:
    void restyle()
    {
        const QString k = property("kind").toString();
        m_border = QColor();
        if (m_pill) {
            if (k == "white")      { m_bg = "#FFFFFF"; m_fg = "#0B1C30"; m_border = QColor("#E4E8F1"); }
            else if (k == "green") { m_bg = "#E3F6EC"; m_fg = "#0B7A4B"; }
            else if (k == "red")   { m_bg = "#FFDAD6"; m_fg = "#93000A"; }
            else if (k == "idle")  { m_bg = "#EEF2FA"; m_fg = "#5A6075"; }
            else                   { m_bg = "#E1EAFF"; m_fg = "#1D4ED8"; }      // tint
        } else {
            if (k == "neutral")    { m_bg = "#EEF2FA"; m_fg = "#434655"; m_border = QColor("#E2E7F1"); }
            else if (k == "white") { m_bg = "#FFFFFF"; m_fg = "#434655"; }
            else if (k == "green") { m_bg = "#E3F6EC"; m_fg = "#0B7A4B"; }
            else                   { m_bg = "#E1EAFF"; m_fg = "#1F4FCF"; }      // blue
        }
        setStyleSheet(QString("color:%1; background:transparent; border:none;").arg(m_fg.name()));
        update();
    }
    bool m_pill;
    QColor m_bg, m_fg, m_border;
};

QLabel *chip(const QString &text, const char *kind) { return new Badge(text, kind, false); }
QLabel *pill(const QString &text, const char *kind) { return new Badge(text, kind, true); }
QLabel *monoPill(const QString &text)
{
    auto *l = new QLabel(text);
    l->setObjectName("monoPill");
    return l;
}

QFrame *card()  { auto *f = new QFrame; f->setObjectName("card"); return f; }
QFrame *panel(const char *name) { auto *f = new QFrame; f->setObjectName(name); return f; }
QFrame *hline() { auto *f = new QFrame; f->setObjectName("hline"); f->setFixedHeight(1); return f; }

QFrame *iconTile(const QString &icon, const QColor &color, int box, int iconSize)
{
    auto *f = new QFrame;
    f->setObjectName("iconTile");
    f->setFixedSize(box, box);
    f->setStyleSheet(QString("QFrame#iconTile { background:#E5EEFF; border:none; border-radius:%1px; }").arg(box / 4));
    auto *l = hbox(f, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0);
    l->setAlignment(Qt::AlignCenter);
    l->addWidget(iconLabel(icon, color, iconSize));
    return f;
}

void setKind(QWidget *w, const char *kind)
{
    w->setProperty("kind", kind);
    w->style()->unpolish(w);
    w->style()->polish(w);
    w->update();
}

void setBadge(QLabel *l, const QString &text, const char *kind)
{
    l->setText(text);
    setKind(l, kind);
}

QPushButton *btn(const QString &text, const char *kind, const QString &icon)
{
    auto *b = new QPushButton((icon.isEmpty() || text.isEmpty()) ? text : " " + text);
    b->setObjectName("btn");
    b->setProperty("kind", kind);
    b->setCursor(Qt::PointingHandCursor);
    if (!icon.isEmpty()) {
        const QString k = kind;
        QColor c("#334155");
        if (k == "primary" || k == "deep") c = QColor("#FFFFFF");
        else if (k == "tint") c = QColor("#1D4ED8");
        else if (k == "link") c = QColor("#004AC6");
        else if (k == "danger") c = QColor("#BA1A1A");
        b->setIcon(makeIcon(icon, c, 18));
        b->setIconSize(QSize(18, 18));
    }
    return b;
}

// ======================================================= mock bars
void drawMockBars(QPainter &p, const QRect &r, const QString &codeIn, const QColor &color)
{
    const QString code = codeIn.isEmpty() ? QString("000000") : codeIn;
    const bool dense = r.width() >= 140;              // small glyphs use fewer, thicker bars
    QVector<int> w = {2, 1, 1, 2, 3, 2};                                    // start
    for (int i = 0; i < code.size(); ++i) {
        int d = code[i].digitValue();
        if (d < 0) d = 0;
        w << (d % 3) + 1 << ((d * 7 + i) % 3) + 1 << ((d + i * 2) % 2) + 1
          << ((d * 5 + 3) % 3) + 1 << ((d + i) % 2) + 1 << ((d * 3 + 1) % 3) + 1
;
        if (dense)
            w << ((d * 2 + i) % 3) + 1 << ((d + i * 3) % 2) + 1 << ((d * 4 + 1) % 3) + 1
              << ((d + 2 * i) % 2) + 1 << ((d * 6 + i) % 3) + 1 << ((d + 1) % 2) + 1;
    }
    w << 2 << 3 << 3 << 1 << 1 << 1 << 2;                                   // stop
    int total = 0;
    for (int v : w) total += v;

    p.save();
    p.setRenderHint(QPainter::Antialiasing, false);
    p.setPen(Qt::NoPen);
    p.setBrush(color);
    const double unit = double(r.width()) / total;
    double x = r.left();
    for (int i = 0; i < w.size(); ++i) {
        const double bw = w[i] * unit;
        if (i % 2 == 0)
            p.drawRect(QRect(qRound(x), r.top(), qMax(1, qRound(x + bw) - qRound(x)), r.height()));
        x += bw;
    }
    p.restore();
}

static QString spaced(const QString &s)
{
    QStringList parts;
    for (QChar c : s) parts << QString(c);
    return parts.join("  ");
}

MiniBarcode::MiniBarcode(const QString &code, QSize size, QWidget *parent) : QWidget(parent), m_code(code)
{
    setFixedSize(size);
}
void MiniBarcode::paintEvent(QPaintEvent *)
{
    QPainter p(this);
    drawMockBars(p, rect(), m_code, QColor("#0B1C30"));
}

// ======================================================= BarcodeView (Generate stage)
BarcodeView::BarcodeView(QWidget *parent) : QWidget(parent)
{
    setMinimumHeight(330);
    setSizePolicy(QSizePolicy::Expanding, QSizePolicy::Expanding);
}
void BarcodeView::setCode(const QString &c)  { m_code = c; m_image = QPixmap(); update(); }
void BarcodeView::setImage(const QPixmap &p) { m_image = p; update(); }
void BarcodeView::setShowDigits(bool on)     { m_digits = on; update(); }
void BarcodeView::setAwaiting(bool on)       { m_awaiting = on; update(); }
void BarcodeView::clear()                    { m_code.clear(); m_image = QPixmap(); update(); }

void BarcodeView::paintBarcodeCard(QPainter &p, const QRect &r) const
{
    p.fillRect(r, Qt::white);
    if (!m_image.isNull()) {
        const QPixmap s = m_image.scaled(r.size() - QSize(40, 24), Qt::KeepAspectRatio, Qt::SmoothTransformation);
        p.drawPixmap(r.center().x() - s.width() / 2, r.center().y() - s.height() / 2, s);
        return;
    }
    const int mx = qRound(r.width() * 0.10);
    const int barH = m_digits ? r.height() - 90 : r.height() - 56;
    drawMockBars(p, QRect(r.left() + mx, r.top() + 28, r.width() - 2 * mx, barH), m_code, QColor("#0B1C30"));
    if (m_digits) {
        QFont f = monoFont(22, true);
        f.setLetterSpacing(QFont::AbsoluteSpacing, 0);
        p.setFont(f);
        p.setPen(QColor("#0B1C30"));
        p.drawText(QRect(r.left(), r.top() + 28 + barH + 8, r.width(), 36), Qt::AlignCenter, spaced(m_code));
    }
}

QPixmap BarcodeView::renderBarcode() const
{
    const QRect base(0, 0, 450, 180);
    QPixmap pm(base.size() * 2);
    pm.fill(Qt::white);
    QPainter p(&pm);
    p.setRenderHint(QPainter::TextAntialiasing);
    p.scale(2, 2);
    paintBarcodeCard(p, base);
    return pm;
}

void BarcodeView::paintEvent(QPaintEvent *)
{
    QPainter p(this);
    p.setRenderHint(QPainter::Antialiasing);

    // stage + dot grid
    QPainterPath stage;
    stage.addRoundedRect(QRectF(rect()), 10, 10);
    p.fillPath(stage, QColor("#EEF3FF"));
    p.setClipPath(stage);
    p.setPen(QPen(QColor("#C9D6EE"), 1.5, Qt::SolidLine, Qt::RoundCap));
    for (int y = 12; y < height(); y += 16)
        for (int x = 12; x < width(); x += 16) p.drawPoint(x, y);
    p.setClipping(false);

    const QColor muted("#737686");
    QFont mf = monoFont(11);
    p.setFont(mf);

    // ruler row (top)
    p.drawPixmap(14, 16, iconPixmap("ruler", muted, 14));
    p.setPen(muted);
    p.drawText(QRect(32, 12, 150, 22), Qt::AlignVCenter | Qt::AlignLeft, "600 px width");
    p.drawText(QRect(width() - 182, 12, 150, 22), Qt::AlignVCenter | Qt::AlignRight, "240 px height");
    p.drawPixmap(width() - 28, 16, iconPixmap("ruler", muted, 14));

    const QString sym = "Active Symbology: CODE 128";
    const int sw = QFontMetrics(mf).horizontalAdvance(sym) + 22;
    const QRect symR((width() - sw) / 2, 11, sw, 24);
    p.setPen(Qt::NoPen);
    p.setBrush(QColor("#E3EAF8"));
    p.drawRoundedRect(symR, 6, 6);
    p.setPen(QColor("#434655"));
    p.drawText(symR, Qt::AlignCenter, sym);

    // bottom row
    p.setBrush(QColor("#0B4BCB"));
    p.setPen(Qt::NoPen);
    p.drawEllipse(QPoint(20, height() - 22), 3, 3);
    p.setPen(muted);
    p.drawText(QRect(30, height() - 34, 220, 22), Qt::AlignVCenter | Qt::AlignLeft, "Optical Density: 99.4%");
    p.drawText(QRect(width() - 262, height() - 34, 246, 22), Qt::AlignVCenter | Qt::AlignRight, "Standard Bumper: Left/Right 12mm");

    // center card
    const int cw = qMin(width() - 80, 450), ch = 236;
    const QRect c((width() - cw) / 2, (height() - ch) / 2, cw, ch);

    if (!hasBarcode() || m_awaiting) {
        p.setPen(QPen(QColor("#9DB4E0"), 1.6, Qt::DashLine));
        p.setBrush(QColor(255, 255, 255, 170));
        p.drawRoundedRect(c, 10, 10);
        QFont f; f.setPixelSize(15); f.setWeight(QFont::DemiBold);
        p.setFont(f);
        p.setPen(QColor("#434655"));
        p.drawText(c.adjusted(0, 0, 0, -22), Qt::AlignCenter, tr("Your barcode will appear here"));
        f.setPixelSize(12); f.setWeight(QFont::Normal);
        p.setFont(f);
        p.setPen(QColor("#737686"));
        p.drawText(c.adjusted(0, 34, 0, 0), Qt::AlignCenter, tr("Enter a 6-digit code and press Generate"));
        return;
    }

    for (int i = 5; i >= 1; --i) {                                          // soft shadow
        p.setPen(Qt::NoPen);
        p.setBrush(QColor(15, 23, 42, 6));
        p.drawRoundedRect(c.adjusted(-i, -i + 4, i, i + 4), 8 + i, 8 + i);
    }
    p.setBrush(Qt::white);
    p.setPen(QPen(QColor("#E4E8F1"), 1));
    p.drawRoundedRect(c, 8, 8);
    p.save();
    p.setRenderHint(QPainter::Antialiasing, false);
    paintBarcodeCard(p, QRect(c.left() + 1, c.top() + 1, c.width() - 2, 180));
    p.restore();

    // footer inside card
    p.setFont(monoFont(11));
    const QString t1 = "Hardware Decodable", t2 = "PAYLOAD: " + m_code;
    const QFontMetrics fm(p.font());
    const int w1 = fm.horizontalAdvance(t1), w2 = fm.horizontalAdvance(t2);
    const int total = 18 + w1 + 22 + w2;
    int x = c.left() + (c.width() - total) / 2;
    const int y = c.top() + 196;
    p.drawPixmap(x, y + 2, iconPixmap("scan", QColor("#0B4BCB"), 14));
    p.setPen(QColor("#0B4BCB"));
    p.drawText(QRect(x + 18, y, w1 + 4, 20), Qt::AlignVCenter, t1);
    p.setBrush(QColor("#C3C6D7")); p.setPen(Qt::NoPen);
    p.drawEllipse(QPoint(x + 18 + w1 + 12, y + 10), 2, 2);
    p.setPen(QColor("#0B1C30"));
    p.drawText(QRect(x + 18 + w1 + 22, y, w2 + 4, 20), Qt::AlignVCenter, t2);
}

// ======================================================= CameraView
CameraView::CameraView(QWidget *parent) : QWidget(parent)
{
    setFixedHeight(352);
    setSizePolicy(QSizePolicy::Expanding, QSizePolicy::Fixed);
    m_timer.setInterval(16);
    connect(&m_timer, &QTimer::timeout, this, [this] { m_phase += 0.03; update(); });   // scan-line animation
}
void CameraView::setRunning(bool on)
{
    m_running = on;
    if (!on) { m_frame = QPixmap(); m_frozen = false; }
    if (on && !m_frozen) m_timer.start(); else m_timer.stop();
    update();
}
void CameraView::setFrozen(bool on)
{
    m_frozen = on;
    if (on) m_timer.stop(); else if (m_running) m_timer.start();
    update();
}
void CameraView::setFrame(const QPixmap &pm)
{
    if (m_frozen) return;
    m_frame = pm;
    update();
}

static int chipWidth(const QFontMetrics &fm, const QString &text, bool dot, bool icon)
{
    return fm.horizontalAdvance(text) + 20 + (dot ? 14 : 0) + (icon ? 20 : 0);
}

static void drawChip(QPainter &p, const QRect &r, const QString &text, const QColor &bg, const QColor &fg,
                     const QColor &dot = QColor(), const QString &icon = QString())
{
    p.setPen(Qt::NoPen);
    p.setBrush(bg);
    p.drawRoundedRect(r, 6, 6);
    int x = r.left() + 10;
    if (dot.isValid()) { p.setBrush(dot); p.drawEllipse(QPoint(x + 3, r.center().y()), 4, 4); x += 14; }
    if (!icon.isEmpty()) { p.drawPixmap(x, r.center().y() - 7, iconPixmap(icon, fg, 14)); x += 20; }
    p.setPen(fg);
    p.drawText(QRect(x, r.top(), r.right() - x, r.height()), Qt::AlignVCenter | Qt::AlignLeft, text);
}

void CameraView::paintEvent(QPaintEvent *)
{
    QPainter p(this);
    p.setRenderHint(QPainter::Antialiasing);
    QPainterPath clip;
    clip.addRoundedRect(QRectF(rect()), 10, 10);
    p.setClipPath(clip);

    QRadialGradient bg(width() / 2.0, height() / 2.0, qMax(width(), height()) * 0.7);
    bg.setColorAt(0, QColor("#10243F"));
    bg.setColorAt(1, QColor("#070F1C"));
    p.fillRect(rect(), bg);

    const bool live = m_running && !m_frame.isNull();
    qreal scale = 1.0;                                                    // widget px per camera px
    if (live) {
        p.setRenderHint(QPainter::SmoothPixmapTransform);
        scale = qMax(qreal(width()) / m_frame.width(), qreal(height()) / m_frame.height());   // "cover" fit
        const QSizeF sz(m_frame.width() * scale, m_frame.height() * scale);
        p.drawPixmap(QRectF((width() - sz.width()) / 2, (height() - sz.height()) / 2, sz.width(), sz.height()),
                     m_frame, QRectF(m_frame.rect()));
        p.fillRect(rect(), QColor(5, 12, 24, 40));                        // slight dim so the HUD stays readable
    } else {
        p.setPen(QPen(QColor(255, 255, 255, 14), 1.5, Qt::SolidLine, Qt::RoundCap));
        for (int y = 14; y < height(); y += 18)
            for (int x = 14; x < width(); x += 18) p.drawPoint(x, y);
    }

    // scan frame (corner brackets)
    const int fw = int(width() * 0.74), fh = int(height() * 0.64);
    const QRect f((width() - fw) / 2, (height() - fh) / 2, fw, fh);
    p.setBrush(Qt::NoBrush);
    p.setPen(QPen(QColor(m_running ? "#2F6BF0" : "#2A3E63"), 4, Qt::SolidLine, Qt::FlatCap, Qt::MiterJoin));
    const int L = 30;
    auto corner = [&](QPoint c, int dx, int dy) {
        p.drawLine(c, c + QPoint(dx * L, 0));
        p.drawLine(c, c + QPoint(0, dy * L));
    };
    corner(f.topLeft() + QPoint(2, 2), 1, 1);
    corner(f.topRight() + QPoint(-2, 2), -1, 1);
    corner(f.bottomLeft() + QPoint(2, -2), 1, -1);
    corner(f.bottomRight() + QPoint(-2, -2), -1, -1);

    p.setFont(monoFont(11));
    const QFontMetrics fm(p.font());

    if (!m_running) {                                                     // camera off
        const QString t = "CAMERA OFF";
        drawChip(p, QRect(14, 14, chipWidth(fm, t, true, false), 26), t, QColor(0, 0, 0, 110), QColor("#CBD5E1"), QColor("#94A3B8"));
        QFont tf; tf.setPixelSize(17); tf.setWeight(QFont::DemiBold);
        p.setFont(tf);
        p.setPen(QColor("#D5DEEE"));
        p.drawText(f.adjusted(0, 0, 0, -20), Qt::AlignCenter, tr("Camera is off"));
        tf.setPixelSize(13); tf.setWeight(QFont::Normal);
        p.setFont(tf);
        p.setPen(QColor("#8493AE"));
        p.drawText(f.adjusted(0, 34, 0, 0), Qt::AlignCenter, tr("Press \"Start Camera\" to begin scanning"));
        return;
    }

    // scan line
    const qreal t = m_frozen ? 0.5 : (qSin(m_phase) + 1) / 2;
    const int y = f.top() + 14 + int(t * (f.height() - 28));
    QLinearGradient glow(0, y - 26, 0, y + 26);
    glow.setColorAt(0, QColor(34, 211, 238, 0));
    glow.setColorAt(0.5, QColor(34, 211, 238, 55));
    glow.setColorAt(1, QColor(34, 211, 238, 0));
    p.fillRect(QRect(f.left(), y - 26, f.width(), 52), glow);
    QLinearGradient ln(f.left(), 0, f.right(), 0);
    ln.setColorAt(0, QColor(34, 211, 238, 0));
    ln.setColorAt(0.5, QColor(103, 232, 249, 255));
    ln.setColorAt(1, QColor(34, 211, 238, 0));
    p.setPen(QPen(QBrush(ln), 2));
    p.drawLine(f.left(), y, f.right(), y);

    // HUD chips (real values from the camera frame)
    p.setFont(monoFont(11));
    QString left = m_frozen ? QString("FROZEN") : QString("LIVE");
    if (live) left += QString(" SENSOR: %1x%2").arg(m_frame.width()).arg(m_frame.height());
    else      left = "CONNECTING...";
    drawChip(p, QRect(14, 14, chipWidth(fm, left, true, false), 26), left, QColor(0, 0, 0, 120), QColor("#E2E8F0"),
             m_frozen ? QColor("#FBBF24") : QColor("#34D399"));
    if (live) {
        const QString roi = QString("ROI: [%1x%2]").arg(qRound(f.width() / scale)).arg(qRound(f.height() / scale));
        const int rw = chipWidth(fm, roi, false, true);
        drawChip(p, QRect(width() - rw - 14, 14, rw, 26), roi, QColor(0, 0, 0, 120), QColor("#CFE0FF"), QColor(), "target");
    }
}

// ======================================================= DropZone
DropZone::DropZone(QWidget *parent) : QFrame(parent)
{
    setAcceptDrops(true);
    setCursor(Qt::PointingHandCursor);
    setFixedHeight(216);
    setSizePolicy(QSizePolicy::Expanding, QSizePolicy::Fixed);

    auto *lay = vbox(this, 20, 16, 20, 16, 6);
    lay->setAlignment(Qt::AlignCenter);

    auto *tile = iconTile("upload", QColor("#0B4BCB"), 56, 26);
    tile->setStyleSheet("QFrame#iconTile { background:#DCE8FF; border:none; border-radius:14px; }");
    lay->addWidget(tile, 0, Qt::AlignHCenter);
    lay->addSpacing(8);

    auto *t = T(tr("Drag & drop a barcode image here or click to browse"), 18, 500, "#0B1C30");
    t->setAlignment(Qt::AlignCenter);
    t->setWordWrap(true);
    lay->addWidget(t);
    auto *s = T(tr("High-contrast raster images yield optimal decoding accuracy"), 14, 400, "#434655");
    s->setAlignment(Qt::AlignCenter);
    s->setWordWrap(true);
    lay->addWidget(s);
    lay->addSpacing(8);

    auto *row = hrow(8);
    row->addStretch();
    for (const char *f : {"PNG", "JPG", "JPEG", "BMP"}) row->addWidget(chip(f, "white"));
    row->addWidget(T("(Up to 10MB)", 11, 400, "#737686", true));
    row->addStretch();
    lay->addLayout(row);
}

void DropZone::paintEvent(QPaintEvent *)
{
    QPainter p(this);
    p.setRenderHint(QPainter::Antialiasing);
    p.setBrush(m_hover ? QColor("#E6EEFF") : QColor("#EFF4FF"));
    p.setPen(QPen(m_hover ? QColor("#0B4BCB") : QColor("#CBDAF7"), 1.5, Qt::DashLine));
    p.drawRoundedRect(QRectF(rect()).adjusted(1, 1, -1, -1), 12, 12);
}

bool DropZone::event(QEvent *e)
{
    if (e->type() == QEvent::Enter) { m_hover = true; update(); }
    else if (e->type() == QEvent::Leave) { m_hover = false; update(); }
    return QFrame::event(e);
}

void DropZone::mousePressEvent(QMouseEvent *)
{
    const QString path = QFileDialog::getOpenFileName(this, tr("Select barcode image"), QString(),
                                                      tr("Images (*.png *.jpg *.jpeg *.bmp)"));
    if (!path.isEmpty()) emit fileChosen(path);
}
void DropZone::dragEnterEvent(QDragEnterEvent *e)
{
    if (e->mimeData()->hasUrls()) { m_hover = true; update(); e->acceptProposedAction(); }
}
void DropZone::dragLeaveEvent(QDragLeaveEvent *) { m_hover = false; update(); }
void DropZone::dropEvent(QDropEvent *e)
{
    m_hover = false; update();
    for (const QUrl &u : e->mimeData()->urls()) {
        const QString suf = QFileInfo(u.toLocalFile()).suffix().toLower();
        if (u.isLocalFile() && (suf == "png" || suf == "jpg" || suf == "jpeg" || suf == "bmp")) {
            emit fileChosen(u.toLocalFile());
            return;
        }
    }
}

// ======================================================= ToggleSwitch
ToggleSwitch::ToggleSwitch(bool on, QWidget *parent) : QWidget(parent), m_on(on)
{
    setFixedSize(44, 24);
    setCursor(Qt::PointingHandCursor);
}
void ToggleSwitch::setChecked(bool on) { if (on != m_on) { m_on = on; update(); emit toggled(on); } }
void ToggleSwitch::mouseReleaseEvent(QMouseEvent *) { setChecked(!m_on); }
void ToggleSwitch::paintEvent(QPaintEvent *)
{
    QPainter p(this);
    p.setRenderHint(QPainter::Antialiasing);
    p.setPen(Qt::NoPen);
    p.setBrush(m_on ? QColor("#2563EB") : QColor("#C3CBDB"));
    p.drawRoundedRect(rect(), 12, 12);
    p.setBrush(Qt::white);
    p.drawEllipse(QPoint(m_on ? width() - 12 : 12, height() / 2), 9, 9);
}

// ======================================================= RadioCard
RadioCard::RadioCard(const QString &title, const QString &sub, QWidget *parent) : QFrame(parent)
{
    setCursor(Qt::PointingHandCursor);
    setMinimumHeight(80);
    auto *v = vbox(this, 14, 12, 40, 12, 3);
    v->addWidget(T(title, 14, 600, "#0B1C30"));
    v->addWidget(T(sub, 11, 400, "#737686", true));
    v->addStretch();
}
void RadioCard::setSelected(bool on) { m_sel = on; update(); }
void RadioCard::mouseReleaseEvent(QMouseEvent *) { emit clicked(); }
void RadioCard::paintEvent(QPaintEvent *)
{
    QPainter p(this);
    p.setRenderHint(QPainter::Antialiasing);
    p.setBrush(m_sel ? QColor("#FFFFFF") : QColor("#E3EBFA"));
    p.setPen(m_sel ? QPen(QColor("#DDE5F5"), 1) : Qt::NoPen);
    p.drawRoundedRect(QRectF(rect()).adjusted(0.5, 0.5, -0.5, -0.5), 8, 8);
    const QPoint c(width() - 22, 26);
    p.setBrush(Qt::NoBrush);
    p.setPen(QPen(m_sel ? QColor("#0B4BCB") : QColor("#8A93A8"), 1.8));
    p.drawEllipse(c, 8, 8);
    if (m_sel) { p.setPen(Qt::NoPen); p.setBrush(QColor("#0B4BCB")); p.drawEllipse(c, 4, 4); }
}

// ======================================================= SegBar
SegBar::SegBar(const QStringList &labels, const QStringList &icons, int current, bool small, QWidget *parent)
    : QFrame(parent), m_icons(icons), m_current(current)
{
    setObjectName("segBar");
    setProperty("small", small);
    auto *l = hbox(this, 4, 4, 4, 4, 4);
    for (int i = 0; i < labels.size(); ++i) {
        auto *b = new QPushButton(icons.value(i).isEmpty() ? labels[i] : "  " + labels[i]);
        b->setObjectName("seg");
        b->setCheckable(true);
        b->setCursor(Qt::PointingHandCursor);
        b->setIconSize(QSize(18, 18));
        connect(b, &QPushButton::clicked, this, [this, i] { setCurrent(i); emit currentChanged(i); });
        l->addWidget(b);
        m_btns << b;
    }
    setCurrent(current);
}

void SegBar::setCurrent(int i)
{
    m_current = i;
    for (int k = 0; k < m_btns.size(); ++k) m_btns[k]->setChecked(k == i);
    refreshIcons();
}

void SegBar::refreshIcons()
{
    for (int k = 0; k < m_btns.size(); ++k) {
        if (m_icons.value(k).isEmpty()) continue;
        m_btns[k]->setIcon(makeIcon(m_icons[k], k == m_current ? QColor("#004AC6") : QColor("#434655"), 18));
    }
}

QFrame *iconChip(const QString &icon, const QString &text, const QString &bg, const QString &fg,
                 const QString &border, bool mono, int px, int radius)
{
    auto *f = new QFrame;
    f->setObjectName("iconChip");
    QString css = QString("QFrame#iconChip { background:%1; ").arg(bg);
    css += border.isEmpty() ? "border:none; " : QString("border:1px solid %1; ").arg(border);
    css += QString("border-radius:%1px; }").arg(radius);        // radius last: a later border shorthand would reset it
    f->setStyleSheet(css);
    auto *l = hbox(f, 10, 5, 12, 5, 7);
    l->addWidget(iconLabel(icon, QColor(fg), 15));
    l->addWidget(T(text, px, 600, fg, mono));
    return f;
}

FILE: /Project Planning/Increment-1.md
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Increment 1 — Barcode Generator and Reader

---

## Overview

The first increment focuses on developing a working version of the C++ desktop application with barcode generation and reading capabilities.

The application must be able to generate a barcode from a six-digit number, display it on the screen, save it as an image, and read the generated barcode through either an uploaded image or a laptop camera.

---

## Requirements

### 1. Barcode Generation

The application must:

- Accept a six-digit numeric input from the user.
- Ensure that the first digit is non-zero.
- Generate a barcode corresponding to the provided number.
- Display the generated barcode on the application screen.
- Save the generated barcode to disk as an image.

### 2. Barcode Reading

The application must be able to read a barcode generated through the application.

**A. Image Upload**

- Allow the user to select and upload a barcode image.
- Read and decode the barcode from the selected image.
- Display the decoded six-digit number on the screen.

**B. Laptop Camera**

- Allow the user to scan a barcode using the laptop camera.
- Detect and decode the barcode from the camera feed.
- Display the decoded six-digit number on the screen.

---

## Technical Constraints

- Existing non-standard libraries must not be used for barcode generation.
- Libraries for image processing, file handling, and other supporting operations are permitted.
- The application must be developed in C++.
- All submissions must be made through GitHub.

---

## Team Contributions

### Farzam — UI Development & Barcode Reading

- UI Development (with Ali)
- Barcode Reading (with Shauzab)
- Barcode Decoding
- Decoded Value Return Handling
- Invalid Input Handling
- File Handling (with Mustafa)

### Ali — UI Development & Barcode Generation

- UI Development (with Farzam)
- Barcode Generation
- Image Processing
- Input Handling
- Taking 6-Digit Number as Input

### Mustafa — Project Lead, Management & Integration

- Project Management
- Project Integration
- Error Handling
- File Handling (with Farzam)
- CMake Build Configuration
- Live Scanner Implementation (with Farzam and Shauzab)

### Shauzab — Camera & Live Scanner Development

- Barcode Reading (with Farzam)
- Live Scanner Implementation
- Camera Opening and Initialization
- Camera Frame Capturing
- Scanner Functionality

---

## Notes

- The generated barcode must represent the original six-digit input.
- Both image upload and laptop camera scanning are required reading methods.
- Further details about the products, files, and their formats will be provided after Increment 1.

# Changelog

### Added
- Established the initial project structure and directory organization.
- Configured CMake as the project build system.
- Added Qt Widgets-based desktop application foundation.
- Implemented the initial application window and navigation.
- Added the Generate Barcode interface for six-digit numeric input.
- Added the Decode Barcode interface with camera and image-upload sections.
- Introduced the initial scan logs interface.
- Organized backend modules for barcode generation, reading, and scanning.
- Added project documentation and contribution guidelines.

- Established the initial foundation of BarcodeHub.
- Some interface elements currently use placeholder data.
- Actual barcode scanning, decoding, and backend integration will be developed in subsequent increments.

FILE: /CMakeLists.txt
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
cmake_minimum_required(VERSION 3.16)

# Skip Qt Creator's automatic vcpkg package manager injection and background maintenance tool check
set(QT_CREATOR_SKIP_VCPKG_SETUP ON CACHE BOOL "Skip Qt Creator vcpkg setup" FORCE)
set(QT_CREATOR_ENABLE_PACKAGE_MANAGER_SETUP OFF CACHE BOOL "Disable Qt Creator package manager setup" FORCE)
set(QT_CREATOR_ENABLE_MAINTENANCE_TOOL_PROVIDER OFF CACHE BOOL "Disable Qt Creator maintenance tool check" FORCE)

# Disable vcpkg manifest mode by default
if(NOT DEFINED VCPKG_MANIFEST_MODE)
    set(VCPKG_MANIFEST_MODE OFF CACHE BOOL "Disable vcpkg manifest mode by default")
endif()

# Normalize CMAKE_TOOLCHAIN_FILE path if defined
if(DEFINED CMAKE_TOOLCHAIN_FILE)
    file(TO_CMAKE_PATH "${CMAKE_TOOLCHAIN_FILE}" CMAKE_TOOLCHAIN_FILE)
elseif(DEFINED ENV{VCPKG_ROOT})
    file(TO_CMAKE_PATH "$ENV{VCPKG_ROOT}/scripts/buildsystems/vcpkg.cmake" _vcpkg_toolchain)
    set(CMAKE_TOOLCHAIN_FILE "${_vcpkg_toolchain}" CACHE STRING "vcpkg toolchain file")
endif()

project(BarcodeHub
    VERSION 0.1.0
    LANGUAGES CXX
)

# C++ Configuration
set(CMAKE_CXX_STANDARD 17)
set(CMAKE_CXX_STANDARD_REQUIRED ON)
set(CMAKE_CXX_EXTENSIONS OFF)

# Qt Configuration (Qt comes from the Qt installer / Qt Creator kit, NOT from vcpkg)
set(CMAKE_AUTOMOC ON)

# Auto-detect Qt installation if not already provided by IDE or environment
if(NOT CMAKE_PREFIX_PATH AND NOT DEFINED ENV{QTDIR})
    file(GLOB _qt_candidates 
        "C:/Qt/6.*/mingw_64" 
        "C:/Qt/6.*/msvc2022_64" 
        "D:/Qt/6.*/mingw_64" 
        "D:/Qt/6.*/msvc2022_64"
    )
    if(_qt_candidates)
        list(GET _qt_candidates -1 _latest_qt)
        list(APPEND CMAKE_PREFIX_PATH "${_latest_qt}")
    endif()
endif()

find_package(QT NAMES Qt6 Qt5 REQUIRED COMPONENTS Widgets)
find_package(Qt${QT_VERSION_MAJOR} REQUIRED COMPONENTS Widgets)

# OpenCV Configuration (automatically detects MinGW or MSVC prebuilt packages)
if(NOT OpenCV_DIR)
    if(DEFINED ENV{OpenCV_DIR})
        set(OpenCV_DIR "$ENV{OpenCV_DIR}")
    elseif(MINGW)
        file(GLOB _opencv_mingw_candidates "C:/opencv-mingw" "C:/opencv/mingw*" "D:/opencv-mingw")
        foreach(_cand IN LISTS _opencv_mingw_candidates)
            if(EXISTS "${_cand}/OpenCVConfig.cmake")
                set(OpenCV_DIR "${_cand}")
                break()
            endif()
        endforeach()
    elseif(MSVC)
        file(GLOB _opencv_msvc_candidates "C:/opencv-msvc" "C:/opencv/build" "D:/opencv/build" "C:/Users/*/Downloads/opencv/build")
        foreach(_cand IN LISTS _opencv_msvc_candidates)
            if(EXISTS "${_cand}/OpenCVConfig.cmake")
                set(OpenCV_DIR "${_cand}")
                break()
            endif()
        endforeach()
    endif()
endif()

find_package(OpenCV QUIET)
if(OpenCV_FOUND)
    message(STATUS "OpenCV found: ${OpenCV_VERSION} (${OpenCV_DIR})")
    add_compile_definitions(HAVE_OPENCV)
else()
    message(STATUS "OpenCV not found. Building without OpenCV support (mock camera mode).")
endif()

# Application Sources
add_executable(BarcodeHub WIN32

    # Frontend Sources
    Frontend/src/main.cpp
    Frontend/src/icons.cpp
    Frontend/src/widgets.cpp
    Frontend/src/generatepage.cpp
    Frontend/src/decodepage.cpp
    Frontend/src/mainwindow.cpp

    # Frontend Headers
    Frontend/include/ui/theme.h
    Frontend/include/ui/icons.h
    Frontend/include/ui/widgets.h
    Frontend/include/ui/generatepage.h
    Frontend/include/ui/decodepage.h
    Frontend/include/ui/mainwindow.h

    # Backend Sources
    Backend/src/barcode/BarcodeGenerator.cpp
    Backend/src/reader/builtin.cpp
    Backend/src/reader/reader.cpp
    Backend/src/scanner/CameraScanner.cpp
    Backend/src/scanner/ImageScanner.cpp

    # Backend Headers
    Backend/include/barcode/BarcodeGenerator.h
    Backend/include/reader/builtin.h
    Backend/include/reader/reader.h
    Backend/include/scanner/CameraScanner.h
    Backend/include/scanner/ImageScanner.h
)

# Include Directories
# NOTE: Frontend/include/ui is required because the sources use flat includes
# like #include "mainwindow.h" while the headers live in Frontend/include/ui/.
target_include_directories(BarcodeHub PRIVATE
    Frontend/include
    Frontend/include/ui
    Backend/include
)

# Link Libraries
target_link_libraries(BarcodeHub PRIVATE
    Qt${QT_VERSION_MAJOR}::Widgets
)

if(OpenCV_FOUND)
    target_include_directories(BarcodeHub PRIVATE ${OpenCV_INCLUDE_DIRS})
    target_link_libraries(BarcodeHub PRIVATE ${OpenCV_LIBS})

    # Automatically copy OpenCV runtime DLLs to output directory on Windows
    if(WIN32 AND OpenCV_LIB_PATH)
        add_custom_command(TARGET BarcodeHub POST_BUILD
            COMMAND ${CMAKE_COMMAND} -E copy_directory_if_different
                "${OpenCV_LIB_PATH}/../bin"
                "$<TARGET_FILE_DIR:BarcodeHub>"
            COMMENT "Copying OpenCV runtime DLLs to output directory..."
        )
    endif()
endif()

# Windows Configuration
if(WIN32)
    set_target_properties(BarcodeHub PROPERTIES
        WIN32_EXECUTABLE TRUE
    )
endif()

# Contributing Guidelines

This document explains how team members can contribute to **Bar-Code-System-CPP**.

## 1. Clone the Repository

Clone the repository and navigate into the project directory:

```bash
git clone https://github.com/Syntrojex/Bar-Code-System-CPP.git
cd Bar-Code-System-CPP
```

## 2. Switch to Your Assigned Branch

Switch to your assigned branch. Replace `your-branch` with your actual branch name.

```bash
git checkout your-branch
```

## 3. Synchronize with Main

Before starting a new task, update your assigned branch with the latest changes from `main`.

```bash
git fetch origin
git merge origin/main
```

Resolve any merge conflicts before continuing.

## 4. Make Your Changes

Add or modify the files required for your assigned task.

Follow the existing project structure and coding conventions. Avoid modifying unrelated files.

## 5. Stage and Commit Changes

Check your changes:

```bash
git status
```

Stage your files:

```bash
git add .
```

Commit your changes with a meaningful message:

```bash
git commit -m "Add barcode generation"
```

## 6. Push Your Branch

Push your changes to your assigned branch:

```bash
git push origin your-branch
```

## 7. Create a Pull Request

Submit your changes for integration into `main`.

1. Open the project repository on GitHub.
2. Select **Compare & pull request**.
3. Set the base branch to `main`.
4. Select your assigned branch as the compare branch.
5. Add a meaningful title and description.
6. Create the Pull Request.

## 8. Review and Merge

The project lead will review and merge Pull Requests into `main`.

Team members should not directly push changes to `main`.

## 9. Start Your Next Task

After your Pull Request has been merged, synchronize your assigned branch with the latest `main` before starting another task.

Repeat the synchronization commands from Section 3, then continue with the same workflow.

## General Guidelines

- Always work on your assigned branch.
- Keep commits meaningful and focused.
- Follow the existing C++ coding conventions.
- Test your changes before submitting a Pull Request.
- Keep Pull Requests limited to the assigned task.
- Coordinate with the project lead when necessary.

Thank you for contributing to the project.

## Ali — Barcode Generation Module: Feature & Verification Log

Project: BarcodeHub / Bar-Code-System-CPP
Branch: ali (local; remote divergence not yet reconciled)
Last verification date: 2026-11-09
Status: Generator implemented and integrated with UI; compiled, displayed, and exported. External scanner interoperability not yet verified.
GEN-001 — Six-digit numeric input validation
- What: Accepts exactly six numeric ASCII digits with first digit 1–9.
- Why: Increment 1 contract requires six-digit payload and excludes leading zero.
- Implementation: QRegularExpression("^[1-9][0-9]{5}$") inside BarcodeGenerator::generate; GeneratePage validates UI entry and disables Generate on invalid input.
- API: BarcodeResult BarcodeGenerator::generate(const QString &number) const returns failure with descriptive error for invalid input.
- Testing: Source inspected and app compiled; invalid-input GUI negative test still pending.
GEN-002 — Manual Code 128 Code Set C encoding
- What: Converts six input digits into three digit pairs and encodes a valid Code 128-C symbol sequence.
- Why: Must produce decodable bars without calling a barcode-generation library.
- Algorithm: Start C (105); three numeric pair symbols; modulo-103 weighted checksum; Stop (106). Example 123456: symbols 105, 12, 34, 56, 44, 106, checksum (105 + 12 + 2*34 + 3*56) % 103 = 44.
- Rendering: Handwritten 107-symbol width-pattern table, alternating bar/space runs painted with Qt QPainter into black-on-white QImage; quiet zones included; digits underneath.
- Code: Backend/src/barcode/BarcodeGenerator.cpp; header Backend/include/barcode/BarcodeGenerator.h unchanged.
- Build evidence: User's cmake --build --preset local-opencv completed and linked BarcodeHub.exe without compilation errors on 2026-11-09.
- Image evidence: User uploaded 123456.png (900×360). Pixel-run inspection of barcode region yielded 37 alternating runs matching Code 128-C symbols 105,12,34,56,44,106 after scaling; checksum verified as 44.
- Limitations: Pixel-rendered export has undergone UI scaling/interpolation; actual interoperability with a separately implemented barcode decoder/hardware scanner remains untested.
GEN-003 — Generator → UI integration and PNG export
- What: GeneratePage::generateRequested is wired in MainWindow to BarcodeGenerator::generate; successful result supplied with GeneratePage::setBarcodeImage(QPixmap::fromImage(result.image)).
- Why: Replace the UI-only mock barcode preview with actual encoded output.
- Code: Frontend/src/mainwindow.cpp signal/slot lambda, existing GeneratePage::onSave and BarcodeView::renderBarcode export path.
- Evidence: User launched BarcodeHub successfully after adding Qt/MinGW DLL directories to terminal PATH, generated and viewed barcode for 123456, saved 123456.png, and uploaded image for inspection.
- Limitations / follow-up: renderBarcode() scales the preview to 900×360 rather than saving the generator's original QImage directly. Improve export to preserve sharp module boundaries, then test independent decoding. Error display path for result.success == false could be improved.
Pending verification
- Test edge-valid payloads (100000, 999999, digits containing 00), leading zero, short/long/non-numeric inputs.
- Independent decode of exported barcode using teammate's scanner or trusted reference implementation.
- Preserve original pixel module widths in PNG export (avoid smooth rescaling); check scanner interoperability.
- Reconcile diverged local/remote ali branch safely before commit/push/PR; preserve teammates' changes.

FILE: /README.md

# Bar-Code-System-CPP

A C++ desktop application designed to simplify inventory management and sales operations for small retail stores, supermarkets, and corner shops.

---

## Project Overview

Bar-Code-System-CPP is an academic term project developed for the **Introduction to Software Engineering (ISE)** course at FAST-NUCES.

The application aims to provide a simple and efficient system through which store employees can manage inventory and record sales with minimal manual input.

Cashiers will be able to scan product barcodes, retrieve product information, add items to orders, and generate bills. Managers will also be able to generate barcodes for products in the store.

The project is being developed incrementally, with a focus on building a reliable, user-friendly, and maintainable desktop application.

---

## Key Features

- **Barcode Generation** — Generate barcodes for products.
- **Barcode Scanning** — Read barcodes using a laptop camera.
- **Image-Based Reading** — Upload barcode images for decoding.

---

## Technology Stack

- **C++** — Core application logic.
- **Qt** — Desktop graphical user interface.
- **CMake** — Build system and project configuration.
- **vcpkg** — C++ dependency management.
- **OpenCV** — Image processing and camera capture.

---

## Project Structure

```text
Bar-Code-System-CPP/
│
├── Frontend/             # Desktop user interface
├── Backend/              # Core application logic
├── Project-Planning/     # Increment-wise project documentation
│
├── CMakeLists.txt        # Build configuration
├── CMakePresets.json     # CMake build presets
├── vcpkg.json            # Dependency manifest
├── .gitignore
└── README.md
```

---

## Development Workflow

The project follows a collaborative GitHub workflow:

1. Each team member works on an assigned branch.
2. Changes are committed and pushed to GitHub.
3. A Pull Request is opened against the `main` branch.
4. Changes are reviewed before being merged into the main project.

---

## Academic Information

| Detail | Information |
|---|---|
| Project | Bar-Code-System-CPP |
| Course | Introduction to Software Engineering (ISE) |
| University | FAST-NUCES |
| Semester | 3rd Semester |
| Instructor | Professor Rana Zeeshan |
| Language | C++ |
| Submission Platform | GitHub |

---

**Bar-Code-System-CPP** — Building a practical retail management solution, one increment at a time.
